    l, g = daten["binodal_liquid"], daten["binodal_vapor"]
    sl, sg = daten["surface_liquid"], daten["surface_vapor"]
    high, mix, q = (daten["surface_high_temperature"], daten["two_phase_surface"], daten["Q_values"])

    for name in ("surface_liquid", "surface_vapor", "surface_high_temperature","binodal_liquid", "binodal_vapor", "two_phase_surface"):
        arr = np.asarray(daten[name])

        if arr.ndim not in (2, 3) or arr.shape[-1] != 3 or not np.isfinite(arr).all():
            raise ValueError(f"u-v-T: ungueltiges Array {name}: {arr.shape}")
        
        if np.any(arr[..., 0] <= 0) or np.any(arr[..., 1] <= 0):
            raise ValueError(f"u-v-T: nichtpositive T/v-Koordinaten in {name}.")
        
    if (l.shape != g.shape or sl.shape[0] != len(l) or sg.shape[0] != len(g) or mix.shape != (len(l), len(q), 3) or high.shape[1] != sl.shape[1] + sg.shape[1] - 1):
        raise ValueError("u-v-T: Teilflaechen haben inkompatible Dimensionen.")
    
    for name, actual, expected in (
        ("Fluessigkeitsrand", sl[:, -1], l),
        ("Dampfrand", sg[:, 0], g),
        ("Zweiphasenrand Q=0", mix[:, 0], l),
        ("Zweiphasenrand Q=1", mix[:, -1], g),
        ("Hochtemperatur-Anschluss", high[0],
         np.concatenate((sl[-1], sg[-1, 1:]), axis=0)),):
        if not np.array_equal(actual, expected):
            raise ValueError(f"u-v-T: Randkontinuitaet verletzt: {name}.")
        
    if not np.array_equal(l[-1], g[-1]) or not np.array_equal(l[-1], np.asarray(daten["critical_point"])):
        raise ValueError("u-v-T: kein gemeinsamer kritischer Endpunkt.")
    
    if (np.any(l[:-1, 1] >= g[:-1, 1]) or not np.all(np.diff(l[:, 0]) > 0)):
        raise ValueError("u-v-T: Binodale thermodynamisch unplausibel.")
    
    if len(q) < 2 or q[0] != 0.0 or q[-1] != 1.0:
        raise ValueError("u-v-T: Dampfgehaltsraster ungueltig.")


def _saturation_uvt_at(state, T):
    """((v_l, u_l), (v_g, u_g)) bei beliebigem T < Tc oder None."""
    try:
        state.update(CP.QT_INPUTS, 0.0, float(T))
        liquid = (1.0 / float(state.rhomolar()), float(state.umass()))
        state.update(CP.QT_INPUTS, 1.0, float(T))
        vapor = (1.0 / float(state.rhomolar()), float(state.umass()))
    except (ValueError, RuntimeError):
        return None
    return liquid, vapor


def _isobare_uvt(state, stoff, p, T_lo, T_hi, n_quality):
    """u-v-T-Isobare aus der p-h-T-Isobare per h-p-Flash (Zustandspunkte, die nicht rueckgerechnet werden: NaN)."""
    hp = calc_isobare(float(p), stoff, T_lo, T_hi, n_T=61, n_quality=n_quality)
    curve = np.full(hp.shape, np.nan, dtype=np.float64)

    for i, (T, h, pressure) in enumerate(hp):
        try:
            state.update(CP.HmassP_INPUTS, float(h), float(pressure))

            if not np.isclose(float(state.T()), T, rtol=0, atol=1e-3):
                raise ValueError("HmassP-Flash ergibt abweichende Temperatur.")

            curve[i] = (float(state.T()), 1.0 / float(state.rhomolar()), float(state.umass()))

        except (ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
            LOGGER.debug("u-v-T-Isobarenpunkt %d: %s", i, exc)

    return curve


def _calculate_isolines(daten, stoff, state, n_quality, n_v, isolines=None):
    """Isolinien entlang geometrisch verteilter Levels (TS-VP03); das Flaechenraster bleibt unveraendert."""
    from isoline_levels import resolve_levels, build_isolines, isotherm_over_volume, P_DOMAIN_FACTORS
    Tc, vc, _ = daten["critical_point"]
    T_sub, T_high = daten["raster"]["T_sub"], daten["raster"]["T_high"]
    T_lo, T_hi = float(T_sub[0]), float(T_high[-1])
    temperatures = np.r_[T_sub, T_high]
    v_min, v_max = daten["meta"]["v_min"], daten["meta"]["v_max"]
    pc = float(state.p_critical())
    levels, report = resolve_levels(isolines, {
        "isothermen": (T_lo, T_hi), "isochoren": (float(v_min), float(v_max)),
        "isobaren": (P_DOMAIN_FACTORS[0] * pc, P_DOMAIN_FACTORS[1] * pc)})

    def isochore(v):
        curve = np.full((len(temperatures), 3), np.nan, dtype=float)
        for i, T in enumerate(temperatures):
            try:
                curve[i] = _u_at_tv(state, T, v)
            except ValueError as exc:
                LOGGER.debug("u-v-T-Isochore: %s", exc)
        return curve

    def isobare(p):
        curve = _isobare_uvt(state, stoff, p, T_lo, T_hi, n_quality)
        outside = ~((curve[:, 1] >= v_min) & (curve[:, 1] <= v_max))
        curve[outside, 1:] = np.nan
        return curve

    curves = build_isolines(levels, report, {
        "isothermen": lambda T: isotherm_over_volume(
            T, Tc, float(v_min), float(v_max), n_v, n_quality, lambda t, v, phase: _u_at_tv(state, t, v),
            lambda t: _saturation_uvt_at(state, t)),
        "isochoren": isochore, "isobaren": isobare}, log=LOGGER.warning)
    daten["isothermen"], daten["isobaren"], daten["isochoren"] = (
        curves["isothermen"], curves["isobaren"], curves["isochoren"])
    daten["isoline_report"] = report


def berechne_uvt_daten(stoff: str = "Water", *, T_min=None, T_max=None,v_min=None, v_max=None, n_T_sub: int = 45, n_T_super: int = 25, n_v: int = 41, n_quality: int = 41, critical_refinement: float = 1.5, include_isolines: bool = True, isolines=None) -> dict:
    """Vollstaendige stabile 3D-u-v-T-Daten, mit separater Zweiphasenflaeche.

    Oberflaechenkoordinaten: (T [K], v_molar [m3/mol], u_mass [J/kg]).
    Metastabile/instabile Zustandszweige und Spinodalen sind nicht enthalten.
    """
    _check_count(n_v, "n_v")
    # p-h-T stellt das bereits erprobte Tc-verdichtete Temperaturraster bereit.
    # n_p=2: es wird KEINE p-h-T-Flache und auch kein Druckraster ausgewertet.
    raster, _ = generate_pht_grid(stoff, T_min=T_min, T_max=T_max, n_T_sub=n_T_sub, n_T_super=n_T_super, n_p=2, n_quality=n_quality, critical_refinement=critical_refinement)
    T_sub, T_high = raster["T_sub"], raster["T_high"]
    state = _new_state(stoff)
    Tc, pc = float(state.T_critical()), float(state.p_critical())
    liquid, vapor = _sat_uvt(stoff, T_sub, state)
    _, vc, uc = liquid[-1]

    # Am fluessigen Rand nur moderat verdichten. Zu kleine v-Werte
    # koennen ausserhalb der HEOS-Modellgueltigkeit liegen.
    default_v_min = 0.985 * float(np.min(liquid[:-1, 1]))
    default_v_max = max(30.0 * vc, 1.2 * float(np.max(vapor[:-1, 1])))
    v_min = default_v_min if v_min is None else float(v_min)
    v_max = default_v_max if v_max is None else float(v_max)

    if (not np.isfinite([v_min, v_max]).all() or not (0 < v_min < np.min(liquid[:, 1]) and v_max > np.max(vapor[:, 1]))):
        raise ValueError("u-v-T: v_min/v_max muessen alle Binodalen einschliessen.")

    n_total = (2 * len(liquid) * n_v + len(T_high) * (2 * n_v - 1) + len(liquid) * n_quality)

    if n_total > MAX_POINTS:
        raise ValueError(f"u-v-T: {n_total:,} Punkte > MAX_POINTS={MAX_POINTS:,}.")

    sl = np.empty((len(liquid), n_v, 3), dtype=np.float64)
    sg = np.empty_like(sl)

    for i, T in enumerate(np.r_[T_sub, Tc]):
        v_l = np.geomspace(v_min, liquid[i, 1], n_v)
        v_g = np.geomspace(vapor[i, 1], v_max, n_v)

        for j, v in enumerate(v_l[:-1]):
            sl[i, j] = _u_at_tv(state, T, v)

        for j, v in enumerate(v_g[1:], start=1):
            sg[i, j] = _u_at_tv(state, T, v)
        # Exakt gleiche Binodal-/Flaechenknoten.
        sl[i, -1] = liquid[i]
        sg[i, 0] = vapor[i]

    high = np.empty((len(T_high), 2 * n_v - 1, 3), dtype=np.float64)
    high[0] = np.concatenate((sl[-1], sg[-1, 1:]), axis=0)
    # dieselben Volumenstuetzstellen bei allen T >= Tc
    for i, T in enumerate(T_high[1:], start=1):
        for j, v in enumerate(high[0, :, 1]):
            high[i, j] = _u_at_tv(state, T, v)

    Q = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_quality)
    T_mix = np.broadcast_to(liquid[:, None, 0], (len(liquid), len(Q)))
    v_mix = ((1.0 - Q[None, :]) * liquid[:, None, 1] + Q[None, :] * vapor[:, None, 1])
    u_mix = ((1.0 - Q[None, :]) * liquid[:, None, 2] + Q[None, :] * vapor[:, None, 2])

    mix = np.stack((T_mix, v_mix, u_mix), axis=-1)
    
    mix[:, 0] = liquid
    mix[:, -1] = vapor
    mix[-1, :] = liquid[-1]  # kritische Zeile ist geometrisch ein Punkt

    result = {
        "surface_liquid": sl,
        "surface_vapor": sg,
        "surface_high_temperature": high,
        "binodal_liquid": liquid,
        "binodal_vapor": vapor,
        "two_phase_surface": mix,
        "Q_values": Q,
        "critical_point": (Tc, vc, uc),
        "raster": raster,
        "meta": {"stoff": stoff, 
                 "backend": "HEOS", 
                 "Tc": Tc, 
                 "pc": pc,
                 "vc": vc, 
                 "uc": uc, 
                 "v_min": v_min, 
                 "v_max": v_max},
        "isothermen": [], 
        "isobaren": [], 
        "isochoren": [],}
    
    validate_uvt_data(result)

    if include_isolines:
        _calculate_isolines(result, stoff, state, n_quality, n_v, isolines)
        
    return result

"""Zentrale, optionale Diagnosen der p-v-T-Anwendung.

Keine Thermodynamik im Produktivpfad, keine Berechnungen beim Import.
Datenkonvention: (T [K], v [m³/mol], p [Pa]).
Importiert weder main noch gui noch visualization noch coolprop_heos.
"""

import logging
import sys
from time import perf_counter

import numpy as np


LOGGER = logging.getLogger("thermosurface.Debug")
ROOT_LOGGER = logging.getLogger("thermosurface")


def configure_debug(enabled):
    """Einziger zentraler Schalter für das anwendungseigene Logging."""
    ROOT_LOGGER.setLevel(logging.DEBUG if enabled else logging.WARNING)
    ROOT_LOGGER.propagate = False
    if not any(getattr(h, "_thermosurface_handler", False) for h in ROOT_LOGGER.handlers):
        handler = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stderr)
        handler.setFormatter(logging.Formatter(
            "%(asctime)s %(levelname)-7s [%(name)s] %(message)s",
            datefmt="%H:%M:%S",
        ))
        handler._thermosurface_handler = True
        ROOT_LOGGER.addHandler(handler)
    LOGGER.debug("Debug-Modus aktiviert")


def diagnose_raster(stoff, raster, visu_raster, meta):
    """Überprüft automatisch ermittelte Raster ohne Debug-Importzyklen."""
    LOGGER.debug("Auto-Raster für %s: Tc=%.8g pc=%.8g vc=%.8g Ttr=%.8g",
                 stoff, meta["Tc"], meta["pc"], meta["vc"], meta["Ttr"])
    LOGGER.debug("Bereich: T=[%.8g, %.8g] K v=[%.8g, %.8g] m³/mol",
                 meta["T_min"], meta["T_max"], meta["v_min"], meta["v_max"])
    if not (meta["T_min"] < meta["Tc"] < meta["T_max"]
            and meta["v_min"] < meta["vc"] < meta["v_max"]):
        raise AssertionError("Kritischer Punkt nicht vollständig innerhalb des Auto-Rasters")
    for groupname, groups in (("Berechnung", raster), ("Darstellung", visu_raster)):
        for name, values in groups.items():
            LOGGER.debug("%s.%s = %s", groupname, name, values)


def timed_call(name, function, *args, **kwargs):
    """Ruft eine bestehende Berechnungsfunktion genau einmal auf."""
    started = perf_counter()
    LOGGER.debug("Berechnung gestartet: %s", name)
    try:
        result = function(*args, **kwargs)
    except Exception:
        LOGGER.exception("Berechnung fehlgeschlagen: %s", name)
        raise
    finally:
        LOGGER.debug("Laufzeit %s: %.4f s", name, perf_counter() - started)
    return result


def _as_points(name, value, ndim=2):
    a = np.asarray(value)
    if a.ndim != ndim or a.shape[-1] != 3:
        raise AssertionError(f"{name}: Erwartet {ndim}D-Array mit letzter Achse 3, erhalten {a.shape}")
    if not np.issubdtype(a.dtype, np.number):
        raise AssertionError(f"{name}: Kein numerischer Datentyp: {a.dtype}")
    finite = np.isfinite(a)
    LOGGER.debug("%s: shape=%s dtype=%s NaN=%d Inf=%d", name, a.shape, a.dtype,
                 int(np.isnan(a).sum()), int(np.isinf(a).sum()))
    if a.size:
        rows_ok = finite.all(axis=-1)
        LOGGER.debug("%s: vollständige Zustände=%d/%d", name,
                     int(rows_ok.sum()), rows_ok.size)
        for j, symbol in enumerate(("T [K]", "v [m³/mol]", "p [Pa]")):
            vals = a[..., j]
            valid = vals[np.isfinite(vals)]
            if valid.size:
                LOGGER.debug("%s %s: min=%.8g max=%.8g", name, symbol, valid.min(), valid.max())
                if np.any(valid <= 0):
                    LOGGER.warning("%s: %s enthält %d Werte <= 0 (logarithmisch ggf. nicht darstellbar)",
                                   name, symbol, int((valid <= 0).sum()))
    return a


def pruefe_isolinien(daten):
    """Überführt die Isolinien-Prüfungen aus der bisherigen main.py."""
    for name, spalte in (("isothermen", 0), ("isochoren", 1)):
        kurven = _as_points(name, daten[name], ndim=3)
        if not kurven.shape[0] or not kurven.shape[1]:
            LOGGER.warning("%s: kein auswertbares Raster", name)
            continue
        konstant = kurven[..., spalte]
        ok = np.allclose(konstant, konstant[:, :1], rtol=1e-12, atol=1e-14)
        LOGGER.debug("%s: konstante Koordinate korrekt=%s", name, ok)
        if not ok:
            raise AssertionError(f"{name}: Isolinien-Koordinate variiert")

    for index, kurve in enumerate(daten["isobaren"]):
        points = _as_points(f"isobaren[{index}]", kurve)
        if not len(points):
            LOGGER.warning("Isobare %d enthält keine Punkte", index)
            continue
        ok = np.allclose(points[:, 2], points[0, 2], rtol=1e-12, atol=1e-5)
        LOGGER.debug("Isobare %d: p konstant=%s", index, ok)
        if not ok:
            raise AssertionError(f"Isobare {index}: Druck nicht konstant")

    liquid = _as_points("binodal_liquid", daten["binodal_liquid"])
    vapor = _as_points("binodal_vapor", daten["binodal_vapor"])
    if liquid.shape != vapor.shape:
        raise AssertionError("Binodale: Äste besitzen keine kompatible Form")
    if len(liquid) < 2:
        LOGGER.warning("Binodale: weniger als zwei gültige Punkte; Zweiphasenfläche ausgelassen")
        return
    np.testing.assert_allclose(liquid[:, 0], vapor[:, 0], rtol=0, atol=1e-8)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(liquid[:, 2], vapor[:, 2], rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-4)
    # Bei einem angehaengten kritischen Punkt ist v_l == v_g zulassig.
    critical_is_last = bool(len(liquid) and np.array_equal(liquid[-1], vapor[-1]))
    last = -1 if critical_is_last else len(liquid)
    if not np.all(liquid[:last, 1] < vapor[:last, 1]):
        raise AssertionError("Unterkritisch muss v_flüssig kleiner als v_dampf sein")
    LOGGER.debug("Binodale: %d passende Flüssig-/Dampfpunkte", len(liquid))


def pruefe_uebereinstimmung(daten):
    """Prüft gemeinsame (T,v)-Stützstellen von Fläche und Isothermen."""
    surface = _as_points("surface", daten["surface"], ndim=3)
    iso = _as_points("isothermen", daten["isothermen"], ndim=3)
    if not len(surface) or not len(iso):
        return
    gemeinsame_T = np.intersect1d(surface[:, 0, 0], iso[:, 0, 0])
    if not gemeinsame_T.size:
        LOGGER.info("Oberfläche/Isothermen: keine gemeinsame Testtemperatur")
        return
    T_test = gemeinsame_T[0]
    surf = surface[np.flatnonzero(np.isclose(surface[:, 0, 0], T_test))[0]]
    line = iso[np.flatnonzero(np.isclose(iso[:, 0, 0], T_test))[0]]
    indices = np.searchsorted(surf[:, 1], line[:, 1])
    contained = indices < len(surf)
    match = np.zeros(len(indices), dtype=bool)
    match[contained] = np.isclose(surf[indices[contained], 1], line[contained, 1], rtol=0, atol=1e-12)
    if not np.any(match):
        LOGGER.info("Oberfläche/Isothermen: keine gemeinsamen Volumenpunkte")
        return
    actual, expected = surf[indices[match], 2], line[match, 2]
    ok = np.allclose(actual, expected, rtol=1e-6, atol=1e-2, equal_nan=True)
    LOGGER.debug("Oberfläche/Isothermen: T=%.5g K gemeinsame v=%d übereinstimmend=%s",
                 T_test, int(match.sum()), ok)
    if not ok:
        raise AssertionError("Isotherme und Fläche liefern unterschiedliche Drücke")


def diagnose_daten(daten):
    """Struktur, Datentypen, physikalische Plausibilität und Phasenmaske."""
    _as_points("surface", daten["surface"], ndim=3)
    _as_points("isothermen", daten["isothermen"], ndim=3)
    _as_points("isochoren", daten["isochoren"], ndim=3)
    for i, kurve in enumerate(daten["isobaren"]):
        _as_points(f"isobaren[{i}]", kurve)
    for name in ("binodal_liquid", "binodal_vapor", "spinodale_liquid", "spinodale_vapor"):
        _as_points(name, daten[name])
    phase_mask = np.asarray(daten["phase_mask"])
    if phase_mask.shape != daten["surface"].shape[:2] or phase_mask.dtype != np.bool_:
        raise AssertionError("phase_mask hat unerwartete Shape oder dtype")
    LOGGER.debug("phase_mask: shape=%s Zweiphasenpunkte=%d", phase_mask.shape, int(phase_mask.sum()))
    liquid = np.asarray(daten["binodal_liquid"])
    vapor = np.asarray(daten["binodal_vapor"])
    two_phase = _as_points("two_phase_surface", daten["two_phase_surface"], ndim=3)

    Q = np.asarray(daten["Q_values"])

    n_sat = two_phase.shape[0]

    if (
        Q.ndim != 1
        or len(Q) != two_phase.shape[1]
        or len(liquid) not in (n_sat, n_sat + 1)
    ):
        raise AssertionError(
            "Zweiphasenfläche und Binodale "
            "besitzen inkompatible Dimensionen."
        )

    if n_sat >= 2:

        np.testing.assert_allclose(
            Q[[0, -1]],
            [0.0, 1.0],
            rtol=0,
            atol=1e-12
        )

        np.testing.assert_allclose(
            two_phase[:, 0, :],
            liquid[:n_sat],
            rtol=1e-10,
            atol=1e-6
        )

        np.testing.assert_allclose(
            two_phase[:, -1, :],
            vapor[:n_sat],
            rtol=1e-10,
            atol=1e-6
        )

        if not (
            np.isfinite(two_phase).all()
            and np.all(two_phase > 0)
        ):
            raise AssertionError(
                "Ungültige Zweiphasenkoordinaten."
            )
        
        # Rand-/Meshpruefungen erfolgen separat in diagnose_visualisierung().
        # Die Variablen surface, transform und critical existieren hier nicht.
        
    Tc, pc, vc = daten["critical_point"]
    if not np.isfinite([Tc, pc, vc]).all() or min(Tc, pc, vc) <= 0:
        raise AssertionError("Kritischer Punkt ist ungültig")
    LOGGER.debug("Kritischer Punkt: Tc=%.8g K pc=%.8g Pa vc=%.8g m³/mol", Tc, pc, vc)
    pruefe_isolinien(daten)
    pruefe_uebereinstimmung(daten)


def test_saturation_plateau(stoff, T, calc_saturation_point, p_eos):
    """Alter Test_1: Sättigungsdruck beim mittleren Zweiphasenvolumen prüfen.

    Thermodynamische Aufrufe werden als bestehende Funktionen übergeben,
    deshalb benötigt Debug.py keinen Import aus coolprop_heos.py.
    """
    sat = calc_saturation_point(T, stoff)
    if sat is None:
        LOGGER.warning("Plateau-Test: kein Sättigungspunkt bei T=%g K", T)
        return None
    liquid, vapor = sat
    p_sat = liquid[2]
    v_mid = 0.5 * (liquid[1] + vapor[1])
    p_test = p_eos(T, v_mid, stoff)
    rel = abs(p_test - p_sat) / p_sat
    LOGGER.debug("Plateau-Test: stoff=%s T=%g K p_sat=%g Pa p(T,v_mid)=%g Pa rel. Fehler=%.3g",
                 stoff, T, p_sat, p_test, rel)
    if not np.isfinite(p_test) or not np.isclose(p_test, p_sat, rtol=1e-3):
        raise AssertionError("HEOS-Sättigungsdruck und DmolarT-Druck stimmen nicht überein")
    return rel


def test_clapeyron(stoff, T):
    """HEOS-Sättigungsableitung gegen Clapeyron (alter Test aus coolprop_heos.py)."""
    import CoolProp.CoolProp as CP
    from CoolProp import AbstractState

    state = AbstractState("HEOS", stoff)
    state.update(CP.QT_INPUTS, 0.0, float(T))
    h_l, v_l = state.hmolar(), 1.0 / state.rhomolar()
    dp_dT_heos = state.first_saturation_deriv(CP.iP, CP.iT)
    state.update(CP.QT_INPUTS, 1.0, float(T))
    h_g, v_g = state.hmolar(), 1.0 / state.rhomolar()
    dp_dT_clapeyron = (h_g - h_l) / (float(T) * (v_g - v_l))
    rel_error = abs(dp_dT_heos - dp_dT_clapeyron) / max(abs(dp_dT_heos), np.finfo(float).tiny)
    LOGGER.debug("Clapeyron: stoff=%s T=%g K HEOS=%g Pa/K Gleichung=%g Pa/K rel. Fehler=%.3g",
                 stoff, T, dp_dT_heos, dp_dT_clapeyron, rel_error)
    if not np.isclose(dp_dT_heos, dp_dT_clapeyron, rtol=1e-5):
        raise AssertionError("Clapeyron-Diagnose fehlgeschlagen")
    return rel_error


def test_spinodale_ableitung(stoff, liquid, vapor, critical, *,
                             tolerance=1e-2, critical_exclusion_rel=1e-3,
                             max_samples=128, state_factory=None, cp_module=None):
    """AP3: Prueft R=|(rho_c/p_c)*(dp/drho_m)_T| dimensionslos.

    Nur unterkritische, endliche homogene Zustaende ausserhalb eines
    relativen kritischen Fensters werden ausgewertet. Die Druckableitung
    wird bei PHASENVORGABE aus HEOS ermittelt (kein Gleichgewichts-Flash).
    Nicht auswertbare Punkte sind keine bestandenen Tests.

    Liefert fuer beide Aeste Zaehler und pass/fail, ohne die GUI zu importieren.
    Durch optionale state_factory/cp_module sind reine Mocktests moeglich.
    """
    if not np.isfinite([tolerance, critical_exclusion_rel]).all() or not (
            tolerance > 0 and 0 < critical_exclusion_rel < 0.1):
        raise ValueError("AP3: Ungueltige Residualtoleranz / kritischer Ausschluss.")
    if type(max_samples) is not int or max_samples < 2:
        raise ValueError("AP3: max_samples muss >=2 sein.")
    Tc, pc, vc = map(float, critical)
    if not np.isfinite([Tc, pc, vc]).all() or min(Tc, pc, vc) <= 0:
        raise ValueError("AP3: Ungueltiger kritischer Punkt.")

    if cp_module is None:
        import CoolProp.CoolProp as cp_module
    if state_factory is None:
        from CoolProp import AbstractState
        state_factory = lambda: AbstractState("HEOS", stoff)
    state = state_factory()
    rho_c = 1.0 / vc
    cutoff = Tc - max(0.5, Tc * critical_exclusion_rel)
    results = {}

    for name, points, phase in (("liquid", liquid, cp_module.iphase_liquid),
                                ("vapor", vapor, cp_module.iphase_gas)):
        arr = np.asarray(points, dtype=float)
        if arr.size == 0:
            arr = np.empty((0, 3), dtype=float)
        if arr.ndim != 2 or arr.shape[1] != 3:
            raise ValueError(f"AP3: {name}-Ast benoetigt (n,3)-Punkte.")
        # Die kritische Endzeile ist absichtlich NICHT testbar: dort liegen
        # numerisch singulaere Grenzverhaeltnisse vor.
        selected = np.flatnonzero(
            np.isfinite(arr).all(axis=1) & (arr[:, 0] > 0)
            & (arr[:, 1] > 0) & (arr[:, 0] < cutoff)
        )
        excluded = int(len(arr) - len(selected))
        n_candidates = int(len(selected))
        if len(selected) > max_samples:
            selected = selected[np.unique(np.linspace(0, len(selected)-1,
                                                        max_samples, dtype=int))]
        residuals = []
        errors = 0
        for index in selected:
            T, v, _p = arr[index]
            try:
                state.specify_phase(phase)
                state.update(cp_module.DmolarT_INPUTS, float(1.0/v), float(T))
                deriv = float(state.first_partial_deriv(
                    cp_module.iP, cp_module.iDmolar, cp_module.iT))
                residual = abs(deriv) * rho_c / pc
                if not np.isfinite(residual):
                    raise ValueError("Nichtendliches Ableitungsresiduum")
                residuals.append(float(residual))
            except (ValueError, RuntimeError, OverflowError) as exc:
                errors += 1
                LOGGER.debug("AP3 %s Ableitung Index %d: %s", name, index, exc)
            finally:
                state.unspecify_phase()
        maximum = float(max(residuals)) if residuals else None
        passed = bool(len(residuals) >= 2 and errors == 0
                      and maximum is not None and maximum <= tolerance)
        results[name] = {
            "candidates": n_candidates, "sampled": int(len(selected)),
            "excluded": excluded, "evaluated": len(residuals), "errors": errors,
            "max_residual": maximum,
            "median_residual": float(np.median(residuals)) if residuals else None,
            "tolerance": float(tolerance), "passed": passed,
        }
        log = LOGGER.info if passed else LOGGER.warning
        log("AP3 Spinodale %s: %s, R_max=%s <= %.3g; %d/%d Stichproben, Fehler=%d, ausgeschlossen=%d",
            name, "BESTANDEN" if passed else "NICHT VALIDIERT",
            f"{maximum:.4g}" if maximum is not None else "n/a", tolerance,
            len(residuals), n_candidates, errors, excluded)
    return results


def diagnose_homogeneous(daten):
    """Numerische Verluste und Oberflaechenzellen ohne VTK protokollieren."""
    from homogeneous_pvt import (count_renderable_triangles, grid_triangles, node_validity,
                                 mesh_topology_report)
    meta = daten.get('homogeneous_validation', {})
    axis = daten.get('pressure_axis', 'log')
    critical = daten.get('critical_point')
    for key in ('metastable_liquid_surface', 'metastable_vapor_surface', 'unstable_surface'):
        surface = np.asarray(daten.get(key, np.empty((0, 0, 3))))
        if surface.ndim != 3 or surface.shape[-1] != 3:
            raise AssertionError(f'{key}: ungueltige Arraystruktur')
        finite = np.isfinite(surface).all(axis=2)
        full = finite.all(axis=1)
        borders = finite[:, 0] & finite[:, -1] if surface.shape[1] else np.zeros(len(surface), dtype=bool)
        n_tri = count_renderable_triangles(surface, axis)
        n_negative = int(np.count_nonzero(finite & (surface[..., 2] <= 0)))
        # NaN-Trennspalten (EOS-Artefakt) sind erlaubt; nur endliche Knoten muessen steigen.
        for row in surface[borders]:
            if not np.all(np.diff(row[np.isfinite(row).all(axis=1), 1]) > 0):
                raise AssertionError(f'{key}: Volumen nicht monoton')
        info = meta.get('surfaces', {}).get(key, {})
        gaps = info.get('gap_rows', {})
        LOGGER.info('%s: vollstaendige=%d partielle=%d EOS-Artefaktzeilen=%d ohne-Rand=%d '
                    'gueltige-Knoten=%d/%d p<=0=%d Dreiecke=%d',
                    key, int(full.sum()), int(np.count_nonzero(borders & ~full)) - len(gaps),
                    len(gaps), int(np.count_nonzero(~borders)), int(finite.sum()),
                    int(finite.size), n_negative, n_tri)
        coverage = info.get('coverage')
        if coverage:
            LOGGER.info('%s: Zellabdeckung=%s (%d/%d auswertbare Zellen), ausgeschlossene '
                        'EOS-Artefaktzellen=%d', key,
                        'n/a' if coverage['coverage'] is None else f"{coverage['coverage']:.2%}",
                        coverage['cells_covered'], coverage['cells_evaluable'],
                        coverage['cells_excluded_gap'])
        if critical is not None and surface.shape[0] > 1:
            Tc, pc, vc = critical
            valid = node_validity(surface, axis)
            xyz = np.stack((np.log10(np.where(valid, surface[..., 1], 1.) / vc),
                            surface[..., 0] / Tc), axis=-1).reshape(-1, 2)
            tri, cells = grid_triangles(valid)
            top = mesh_topology_report(tri, cells, valid, xy=xyz)
            LOGGER.info('%s: Dreiecke=%d Komponenten=%d offene Kanten (Rand/Luecke/unerklaert)='
                        '%d/%d/%d Orientierungskonflikte=%d Projektionsfaltungen=%d', key,
                        top['triangles'], top['components'], top['open_edges_frame'],
                        top['open_edges_gap'], top['open_edges_unexplained'],
                        top['orientation_conflicts'], top['projected_orientation_flips'])
            if top['open_edges_unexplained'] or top['orientation_conflicts'] \
                    or top['projected_orientation_flips']:
                LOGGER.warning('%s: Netzfehler erkannt: %s', key, top)
        for i, errors in info.get('point_reasons', {}).items():
            LOGGER.debug('%s Temperaturindex %s: verworfene Punkte %s', key, i, errors)
        for i, reason in info.get('reasons', {}).items():
            LOGGER.warning('%s Temperaturindex %s: Randproblem %s', key, i, reason)
        for i, gap in gaps.items():
            LOGGER.debug('%s Temperaturindex %s: EOS-Artefaktluecke v in (%s, %s), p-Scan '
                         '[%s, %s], Vorzeichenwechsel=%s', key, i, gap.get('v_a'), gap.get('v_b'),
                         gap.get('scan_p_min'), gap.get('scan_p_max'),
                         gap.get('slope_sign_changes'))
        check = info.get('independent_check')
        if check and not check.get('passed', True):
            LOGGER.warning('%s: unabhaengige Knotenpruefung fehlgeschlagen: %s', key, check)
    combined = meta.get('combined')
    if combined:
        LOGGER.info('Zusammengefuehrte Flaeche: Komponenten=%s (erwartet %s), offene Kanten zwischen '
                    'gemeinsamen Knoten=%s', combined.get('components'),
                    combined.get('components_expected'),
                    combined.get('open_edges_between_shared_nodes'))
    if daten.get('critical_closure'):
        LOGGER.info('Kritischer Punkt: %s', daten['critical_closure'])
    if meta.get('error'):
        LOGGER.warning('Homogene Berechnung fehlgeschlagen: %s', meta['error'])
    return meta


def diagnose_visualisierung(daten, actors, transform):
    """Pruft zweiphasige Mesh-Geometrie bei optionalem kritischem Binodal-Endpunkt."""
    import pyvista as pv

    required = {"surface", "isothermen", "isochoren", "isobaren", "binodale",
                "spinodale", "zweiphasengebiet", "critical_point"}
    missing = required - set(actors)
    if missing:
        raise AssertionError(f"Darstellungs-Ebenen fehlen: {missing}")
    for key, actor in actors.items():
        amount = len(actor) if isinstance(actor, (list, tuple)) else int(actor is not None)
        LOGGER.debug("Visualisierung %s: Actor-Anzahl=%d", key, amount)

    liquid = np.asarray(daten["binodal_liquid"], dtype=float)
    vapor = np.asarray(daten["binodal_vapor"], dtype=float)
    surface = np.asarray(daten["two_phase_surface"], dtype=float)
    critical = daten["critical_point"]  # (Tc, pc, vc)!

    if surface.shape == (0, 0, 3):
        LOGGER.debug("Visualisierung: keine Zweiphasenfläche verfügbar")
        return
    if surface.ndim != 3 or surface.shape[-1] != 3 or min(surface.shape[:2]) < 2:
        raise AssertionError("Zweiphasenfläche braucht (n_T>=2, n_Q>=2, 3).")
    n_T, n_Q = surface.shape[:2]
    if liquid.shape != vapor.shape or liquid.shape not in ((n_T, 3), (n_T + 1, 3)):
        raise AssertionError("Binodalarrays benötigen n_T oder n_T+1 Zeilen.")

    # Die letzte Binodalzeile kann der kritische Punkt sein, während die
    # Zweiphasenfläche nur die unterkritischen Profile enthält.
    np.testing.assert_allclose(surface[:, 0, :], liquid[:n_T], rtol=1e-10, atol=1e-12)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(surface[:, -1, :], vapor[:n_T], rtol=1e-10, atol=1e-12)

    if len(liquid) == n_T + 1:
        Tc, pc, vc = critical
        endpoint = np.array([Tc, vc, pc], dtype=float)
        np.testing.assert_allclose(liquid[-1], endpoint, rtol=1e-12, atol=1e-12)
        np.testing.assert_allclose(vapor[-1], endpoint, rtol=1e-12, atol=1e-12)
        if not surface[-1, 0, 0] < Tc:
            raise AssertionError("Letzte Q-Zeile muss unterhalb Tc liegen.")

    xyz = transform(surface, critical)
    if not np.isfinite(xyz).all():
        raise AssertionError("Nichtendliche Zweiphasenkoordinaten")
    mesh = pv.StructuredGrid(xyz[..., 0], xyz[..., 1], xyz[..., 2])
    if mesh.n_points != n_T * n_Q or mesh.n_cells != (n_T - 1) * (n_Q - 1):
        raise AssertionError("Geometrischer Mesh-Test fehlgeschlagen")
    areas = np.asarray(mesh.compute_cell_sizes().cell_data["Area"])
    if not np.isfinite(areas).all() or not np.all(areas > 0):
        raise AssertionError("Mesh enthält ungültige oder entartete Flächen")
    LOGGER.debug("Zweiphasen-Mesh: Punkte=%d Zellen=%d Flächen min/max=%g/%g",
                 mesh.n_points, mesh.n_cells, areas.min(), areas.max())


def create_test_data():
    """Synthetischer Raster-Datensatz für unabhängige Koordinatentests (kein HEOS)."""

    T = np.linspace(400.0, 600.0, 9)
    v = np.geomspace(2e-5, 5e-4, 15)

    T_grid, V_grid = np.meshgrid(T, v, indexing="ij")

    P = 5e6 * (T_grid / 500.0) * (1e-4 / V_grid)

    surface = np.stack(
        (T_grid, V_grid, P),
        axis=-1
    )

    return {
        "surface": surface,
        "isothermen": surface[::4, :, :],
        "isochoren": np.swapaxes(surface[:, ::5, :], 0, 1),
        "isobaren": [],
        "binodal_liquid": np.empty((0, 3)),
        "binodal_vapor": np.empty((0, 3)),
        "critical_point": (500.0, 5e6, 1e-4)
    }

"""
Datei: diagram_axes.py
Projekt: ThermoSurface
Kategorie: Visualisierung / Achsenlogik

Beschreibung:
Definiert die physikalischen Achsen und Eingabeeinheiten der sieben Diagramme.

Aufgaben:
- Ordnet Darstellungsachsen den Zustandsgrößen und Skalierungen zu.
- Prüft Viewportgrenzen und berechnet Tickwerte und Koordinaten.
Intern SI; Achsentitel und Eingaben verwenden ausgewiesene Anzeigeeinheiten.

Abhängigkeiten:
- Projektmodule (direkt): diagram_coords.py, zustandsflaeche.py
- Bibliotheken: numpy

Verwendet von:
- Projektmodule: axes_view.py, diagram_viewport.py, gui.py
"""

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass

import numpy as np

import diagram_coords as dc
from zustandsflaeche import PRESSURE_AXES


class ViewportBoundsError(ValueError):
    """Ungueltige manuelle Viewportgrenzen (Validierungsmeldung fuer die GUI)."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AxisSpec:
    key: str          # T, v, p, h, s, u, g, f
    name: str         # Klartextname
    unit: str         # Anzeigeeinheit
    scale: float      # Anzeigewert = SI-Wert * scale
    column: int       # Spalte der nativen Zustandsdaten dieses Diagramms
    positive: bool    # physikalisch nur > 0 zulaessig (T, v)

    @property
    def title(self):
        return f"{self.key} [{self.unit}]"


def _ax(key, column):
    name, unit, scale, positive = {
        "T": ("Temperatur", "K", 1.0, True),
        "v": ("Molares Volumen", "m³/mol", 1.0, True),
        "p": ("Druck", "MPa", 1e-6, False),
        "h": ("Spezifische Enthalpie", "kJ/kg", 1e-3, False),
        "s": ("Spezifische Entropie", "kJ/(kg K)", 1e-3, False),
        "u": ("Spezifische innere Energie", "kJ/kg", 1e-3, False),
        "g": ("Spezifische freie Enthalpie", "kJ/kg", 1e-3, False),
        "f": ("Spezifische freie Energie", "kJ/kg", 1e-3, False),
    }[key]
    return AxisSpec(key, name, unit, scale, column, positive)


# Koordinatenvertrag (TS-VP01, Abschn. 1.3): Darstellungsachsen (x, y, z) -> Zustandsgroesse und Spalte der
# nativen Daten. Native Spaltenreihenfolge: pvt (T,v,p), pht (T,h,p), pst (T,s,p), hsp (h,s,p), uvt (T,v,u),
# gpt (T,p,g), fvt (T,v,f).
DIAGRAM_AXES = {
    "pvt": (_ax("v", 1), _ax("T", 0), _ax("p", 2)),
    "pht": (_ax("h", 1), _ax("T", 0), _ax("p", 2)),
    "pst": (_ax("s", 1), _ax("T", 0), _ax("p", 2)),
    "hsp": (_ax("h", 0), _ax("s", 1), _ax("p", 2)),
    "uvt": (_ax("v", 1), _ax("T", 0), _ax("u", 2)),
    "gpt": (_ax("p", 1), _ax("T", 0), _ax("g", 2)),
    "fvt": (_ax("v", 1), _ax("T", 0), _ax("f", 2)),
}
AXIS_LETTERS = ("x", "y", "z")


def axes_for(diagram_id):
    try:
        return DIAGRAM_AXES[diagram_id]
    except KeyError:
        raise ValueError(f"Unbekannter Diagrammtyp: {diagram_id}") from None


def _native_reference(diagram_id, daten):
    """Kritischer Zustand in nativer Spaltenreihenfolge (Fuellwerte fuer die nicht variierten Spalten)."""
    c = daten["critical_point"]
    return {"pvt": (c[0], c[2], c[1]), "pht": (c[0], c[2], c[1]), "pst": (c[0], c[2], c[1]), "hsp": tuple(c),
            "uvt": tuple(c), "gpt": tuple(c), "fvt": tuple(c)}[diagram_id]


def pressure_axis_of(daten):
    axis = (daten or {}).get("pressure_axis", "log")
    return axis if axis in PRESSURE_AXES else "log"


def axis_scale(diagram_id, index, daten):
    """Skalenart der Darstellungsachse `index`: 'linear' | 'log' | 'asinh' | 'symlog'."""
    spec = axes_for(diagram_id)[index]
    if spec.key == "v":
        return "log"
    if spec.key == "p":
        return pressure_axis_of(daten) if diagram_id in ("pvt", "gpt") else "log"
    return "linear"


def _forward(diagram_id, daten, points):
    critical = daten["critical_point"]
    if diagram_id == "pvt":
        return dc.to_xyz(points, critical, pressure_axis_of(daten))
    if diagram_id == "gpt":
        return dc.to_xyz_gpt(points, critical, pressure_axis_of(daten))
    return {"pht": dc.to_xyz_pht, "pst": dc.to_xyz_pst, "hsp": dc.to_xyz_hsp, "uvt": dc.to_xyz_uvt,
            "fvt": dc.to_xyz_fvt}[diagram_id](points, critical)


def _inverse(diagram_id, daten, xyz):
    critical = daten["critical_point"]
    if diagram_id == "pvt":
        return dc.from_xyz(xyz, critical, pressure_axis_of(daten))
    if diagram_id == "gpt":
        return dc.from_xyz_gpt(xyz, critical, pressure_axis_of(daten))
    return {"pht": dc.from_xyz_pht, "pst": dc.from_xyz_pst, "hsp": dc.from_xyz_hsp, "uvt": dc.from_xyz_uvt,
            "fvt": dc.from_xyz_fvt}[diagram_id](xyz, critical)


def to_display(diagram_id, daten, index, values_si):
    """SI-Werte der Achse `index` -> Darstellungskoordinate (dieselbe Vorwaertstransformation wie die Meshes)."""
    spec = axes_for(diagram_id)[index]
    values = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(values_si, dtype=float))
    points = np.tile(np.asarray(_native_reference(diagram_id, daten), dtype=float), (len(values), 1))
    points[:, spec.column] = values
    with np.errstate(all="ignore"):
        return _forward(diagram_id, daten, points)[:, index]


def to_physical(diagram_id, daten, index, coords):
    """Darstellungskoordinaten der Achse `index` -> SI-Werte (Umkehrung von `to_display`)."""
    spec = axes_for(diagram_id)[index]
    coords = np.atleast_1d(np.asarray(coords, dtype=float))
    xyz = np.zeros((len(coords), 3))
    reference = _forward(diagram_id, daten, np.asarray([_native_reference(diagram_id, daten)], dtype=float))[0]
    xyz[:] = reference
    xyz[:, index] = coords
    with np.errstate(all="ignore"):
        return _inverse(diagram_id, daten, xyz)[:, spec.column]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Grenzenpruefung

def validate_bounds(diagram_id, daten, bounds_display):
    """Prueft sechs Grenzen in ANZEIGEEINHEITEN: [(lo, hi)_x, (lo, hi)_y, (lo, hi)_z].

    Rueckgabe: (bounds_si, box) mit bounds_si = [(lo, hi), ...] in SI und box = (xmin, xmax, ymin, ymax, zmin, zmax)
    in Darstellungskoordinaten. Wirft ViewportBoundsError mit lesbarer Meldung.
    """
    specs = axes_for(diagram_id)
    bounds_si, box = [], []
    for index, ((lo, hi), spec) in enumerate(zip(bounds_display, specs)):
        letter = AXIS_LETTERS[index]
        label = f"{letter}-Achse ({spec.key})"
        for name, value in (("Start", lo), ("Stop", hi)):
            if value is None or not math.isfinite(value):
                raise ViewportBoundsError(f"{label}: {name} muss eine endliche Zahl sein.")
        if not lo < hi:
            raise ViewportBoundsError(f"{label}: Start ({lo:g}) muss kleiner als Stop ({hi:g}) sein.")
        lo_si, hi_si = lo / spec.scale, hi / spec.scale
        scale_kind = axis_scale(diagram_id, index, daten)
        if spec.positive and lo_si <= 0:
            raise ViewportBoundsError(f"{label}: {spec.name} muss > 0 sein (Start = {lo:g} {spec.unit}).")
        if scale_kind == "log" and lo_si <= 0:
            raise ViewportBoundsError(
                f"{label}: Logarithmische Achse erfordert Start > 0 (Start = {lo:g} {spec.unit}); fuer p <= 0 "
                "pressure_axis 'asinh', 'symlog' oder 'linear' verwenden.")
        coords = to_display(diagram_id, daten, index, [lo_si, hi_si])
        if not np.isfinite(coords).all() or not coords[0] < coords[1]:
            raise ViewportBoundsError(f"{label}: Grenzen {lo:g}..{hi:g} {spec.unit} sind nicht darstellbar.")
        bounds_si.append((lo_si, hi_si))
        box.extend((float(coords[0]), float(coords[1])))
    return bounds_si, tuple(box)


def bounds_to_box(diagram_id, daten, bounds_si):
    """Gespeicherte SI-Grenzen -> VTK-Box (xmin, xmax, ymin, ymax, zmin, zmax) fuer die aktuellen Daten."""
    box = []
    for index, (lo, hi) in enumerate(bounds_si):
        coords = to_display(diagram_id, daten, index, [lo, hi])
        if not np.isfinite(coords).all() or not coords[0] < coords[1]:
            raise ViewportBoundsError(
                f"Gespeicherte Grenzen der {AXIS_LETTERS[index]}-Achse sind fuer die aktuellen Daten nicht darstellbar.")
        box.extend((float(coords[0]), float(coords[1])))
    return tuple(box)


def display_bounds_to_physical(diagram_id, daten, box):
    """VTK-Box -> physikalische Grenzen in Anzeigeeinheiten [(lo, hi)_x, (lo, hi)_y, (lo, hi)_z]."""
    out = []
    for index, spec in enumerate(axes_for(diagram_id)):
        lo, hi = to_physical(diagram_id, daten, index, [box[2 * index], box[2 * index + 1]]) * spec.scale
        out.append((float(lo), float(hi)))
    return out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Ticks (physikalische Werte)

def format_tick(value):
    if value == 0:
        return "0"
    magnitude = abs(value)
    if magnitude >= 1e6 or magnitude < 1e-3:
        mantissa, exponent = f"{value:.4e}".split("e")
        mantissa = mantissa.rstrip("0").rstrip(".")
        return f"{mantissa}e{int(exponent)}"
    return f"{value:.6g}"


def _linear_ticks(lo, hi, target):
    span = hi - lo
    if not span > 0:
        return [lo]
    raw = span / max(target, 1)
    exponent = math.floor(math.log10(raw))
    fraction = raw / 10.0 ** exponent
    step = next(m for m in (1.0, 2.0, 2.5, 5.0, 10.0) if fraction <= m + 1e-12) * 10.0 ** exponent
    first = math.ceil(lo / step - 1e-9)
    last = math.floor(hi / step + 1e-9)
    return [k * step for k in range(first, last + 1)]


def _decade_ticks(lo, hi, mantissas):
    values = []
    for exponent in range(math.floor(math.log10(lo)) - 1, math.ceil(math.log10(hi)) + 2):
        for m in mantissas:
            value = m * 10.0 ** exponent
            if lo * (1 - 1e-12) <= value <= hi * (1 + 1e-12):
                values.append(value)
    return values


def _thin(values, limit):
    values = list(values)
    while len(values) > limit:
        values = values[::2]
    return values


def physical_ticks(scale_kind, lo, hi, target=6, min_abs=0.0):
    """Tickwerte (in Anzeigeeinheiten) fuer den physikalischen Bereich [lo, hi] der Achse.

    linear: 1-2-2.5-5-Raster; log: Dekaden (bei weniger als 3 Dekaden zusaetzlich 2 und 5);
    asinh/symlog: 0 sowie symmetrische Dekaden bzw. 1-2-5-Werte der positiven und negativen Seite; `min_abs`
    (Anzeigeeinheiten) blendet Ticks im nahezu linearen Bereich um 0 aus, die dort optisch zusammenfielen.
    """
    if not (math.isfinite(lo) and math.isfinite(hi)) or not lo < hi:
        return []
    if scale_kind == "linear":
        return _linear_ticks(lo, hi, target)
    if scale_kind == "log":
        if lo <= 0:
            return []
        values = _decade_ticks(lo, hi, (1.0,))
        if len(values) < 3:
            values = _decade_ticks(lo, hi, (1.0, 2.0, 5.0))
        return _thin(values, 2 * target)
    if scale_kind in ("asinh", "symlog"):
        positive_hi = hi if hi > 0 else 0.0
        negative_hi = -lo if lo < 0 else 0.0
        floor_value = max(min_abs, 1e-30)

        def candidates(mantissas):
            pos = _decade_ticks(floor_value, positive_hi, mantissas) if positive_hi > floor_value else []
            neg = [-x for x in _decade_ticks(floor_value, negative_hi, mantissas)] if negative_hi > floor_value else []
            return sorted(neg + ([0.0] if lo <= 0 <= hi else []) + pos)

        values = candidates((1.0,))
        if len([x for x in values if x != 0.0]) < 5:
            values = candidates((1.0, 2.0, 5.0))
        zero = [0.0] if 0.0 in values else []
        rest = [x for x in values if x != 0.0]
        while len(rest) + len(zero) > 2 * target:
            rest = rest[::2]
        return sorted(rest + zero)
    raise ValueError(f"Unbekannte Skalenart: {scale_kind}")


def tick_floor(diagram_id, index, daten):
    """Kleinster angezeigter |Tick| (Anzeigeeinheiten) fuer asinh/symlog-Druckachsen, sonst 0."""
    spec = axes_for(diagram_id)[index]
    if spec.key == "p" and axis_scale(diagram_id, index, daten) in ("asinh", "symlog"):
        return 1e-2 * float(daten["critical_point"][1]) * spec.scale
    return 0.0


def axis_title(diagram_id, index, daten):
    spec = axes_for(diagram_id)[index]
    kind = axis_scale(diagram_id, index, daten)
    suffix = {"linear": "", "log": " (log)", "asinh": " (asinh)", "symlog": " (symlog)"}[kind]
    return spec.title + suffix

"""
Datei: diagram_cache.py
Projekt: ThermoSurface
Kategorie: Hilfsmodul / Cache

Beschreibung:
Speichert berechnete Diagrammdatensätze in einem speicherbegrenzten RAM-Cache.

Aufgaben:
- Erzeugt stabile Cache-Schlüssel aus Stoff, Modell und Rechenparametern.
- Verdrängt ältere Einträge nach LRU-Prinzip und meldet Speichergrenzen.

Abhängigkeiten:
- Projektmodule (direkt): keine
- Bibliotheken: CoolProp, numpy

Verwendet von:
- Projektmodule: calculation_manager.py, gui.py
"""

import hashlib
import json
import logging
from collections import OrderedDict
from dataclasses import dataclass
from threading import RLock

import numpy as np

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("thermosurface.cache")

MODEL = "heos"
SCHEMA_VERSION = 1


def solver_version():
    try:
        import CoolProp
        return f"CoolProp {CoolProp.__version__}|schema {SCHEMA_VERSION}"
    except Exception:
        return f"CoolProp n/a|schema {SCHEMA_VERSION}"


def _canonical(value):
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return {str(k): _canonical(v) for k, v in sorted(value.items(), key=lambda item: str(item[0]))}
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return [_canonical(v) for v in value]
    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
        return {"__ndarray__": hashlib.sha1(np.ascontiguousarray(value).tobytes()).hexdigest(),
                "shape": list(value.shape), "dtype": str(value.dtype)}
    if isinstance(value, (np.floating, np.integer, np.bool_)):
        return value.item()
    if isinstance(value, float):
        return repr(value)
    return value


def params_hash(params):
    text = json.dumps(_canonical(params), sort_keys=True, default=repr)
    return hashlib.sha1(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DiagramKey:
    fluid: str
    diagram_id: str
    params_hash: str
    model: str = MODEL
    solver_version: str = ""

    @classmethod
    def build(cls, fluid, diagram_id, params):
        return cls(str(fluid), str(diagram_id), params_hash(params), MODEL, solver_version())


def estimate_nbytes(value, _seen=None):
    """Grobe Speicherabschaetzung (numpy-Arrays zaehlen exakt, sonst rekursiv/Objekt-__dict__)."""
    _seen = set() if _seen is None else _seen
    if id(value) in _seen:
        return 0
    _seen.add(id(value))
    if isinstance(value, np.ndarray):
        return int(value.nbytes)
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        return sum(estimate_nbytes(v, _seen) for v in value.values())
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, set)):
        return sum(estimate_nbytes(v, _seen) for v in value)
    if hasattr(value, "__dict__"):
        return estimate_nbytes(vars(value), _seen)
    return 64


class DiagramResultCache:
    """LRU-Cache. Bei Ueberschreitung des Budgets werden aelteste Eintraege verdraengt und gemeldet
    (nie stillschweigend); der zuletzt gespeicherte Eintrag bleibt immer erhalten."""

    def __init__(self, max_bytes=2 * 1024 ** 3, warn_bytes=None):
        self.max_bytes = int(max_bytes)
        self.warn_bytes = int(warn_bytes) if warn_bytes is not None else int(0.75 * self.max_bytes)
        self._items = OrderedDict()
        self._sizes = {}
        self._lock = RLock()
        self.hits = 0
        self.misses = 0
        self.evictions = []
        self.warnings = []

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self._items)

    def __contains__(self, key):
        return key in self._items

    @property
    def total_bytes(self):
        return sum(self._sizes.values())

    def get(self, key):
        with self._lock:
            if key in self._items:
                self._items.move_to_end(key)
                self.hits += 1
                return self._items[key]
            self.misses += 1
            return None

    def peek(self, key):
        return self._items.get(key)

    def put(self, key, daten):
        """Speichert `daten`; gibt Liste der dabei verdraengten Schluessel zurueck."""
        with self._lock:
            self._items[key] = daten
            self._items.move_to_end(key)
            self._sizes[key] = estimate_nbytes(daten)
            evicted = []
            while self.total_bytes > self.max_bytes and len(self._items) > 1:
                old, _ = next(iter(self._items.items()))
                if old == key:
                    break
                del self._items[old]
                self._sizes.pop(old, None)
                evicted.append(old)
            if evicted:
                self.evictions.extend(evicted)
                LOGGER.warning("Cache-Budget %.0f MB ueberschritten: %d Ergebnis(se) verdraengt (werden bei Bedarf neu berechnet)",
                               self.max_bytes / 1e6, len(evicted))
            if self.total_bytes > self.warn_bytes:
                message = (f"Cache belegt {self.total_bytes / 1e6:.0f} MB "
                           f"(Warnschwelle {self.warn_bytes / 1e6:.0f} MB, Budget {self.max_bytes / 1e6:.0f} MB)")
                self.warnings.append(message)
                LOGGER.warning(message)
            return evicted

    def clear(self):
        with self._lock:
            self._items.clear()
            self._sizes.clear()

    def stats(self):
        return {"entries": len(self._items), "bytes": self.total_bytes, "hits": self.hits,
                "misses": self.misses, "evictions": len(self.evictions)}

"""
Datei: diagram_coords.py
Projekt: ThermoSurface
Kategorie: Visualisierung / Koordinaten

Beschreibung:
Transformiert Zustandsgrößen der sieben Diagramme in 3D-Anzeigekoordinaten.

Aufgaben:
- Stellt Vorwärts- und Rücktransformationen für die Diagrammtypen bereit.
- Berücksichtigt normierte sowie logarithmische Druck- und Volumenachsen.

Abhängigkeiten:
- Projektmodule (direkt): zustandsflaeche.py
- Bibliotheken: numpy

Verwendet von:
- Projektmodule: diagram_axes.py, diagram_viewport.py, fvt_tangent.py, visualization.py
"""

import numpy as np

from zustandsflaeche import PRESSURE_AXES, pressure_to_z, z_to_pressure


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Vorwaertstransformationen

def to_xyz(points, critical, pressure_axis="log"):
    """Einheitliche p-v-T-Koordinaten fuer ALLE Actors, inklusive Phasengrenzen.

    p_raw bleibt unveraendert; nur die Darstellungskoordinate z wird transformiert
    (zustandsflaeche.pressure_to_z: log nur fuer p>0, asinh/symlog/linear fuer alle p).
    """
    Tc, pc, vc = critical
    pts = np.asarray(points, dtype=float)
    if pressure_axis not in PRESSURE_AXES:
        raise ValueError("pressure_axis muss eines von " + ", ".join(PRESSURE_AXES) + " sein")
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore", over="ignore"):
        z = pressure_to_z(pts[..., 2], pc, pressure_axis)
        return np.stack((np.log10(pts[..., 1] / vc), pts[..., 0] / Tc, z), axis=-1)


def to_xyz_pht(points, critical):
    """(T, h, p) -> PyVista-XYZ; critical: (Tc, pc, hc)."""
    Tc, pc, hc = critical
    points = np.asarray(points, dtype=float)
    T = points[..., 0]
    h = points[..., 1]
    p = points[..., 2]
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        return np.stack(((h - hc) / 1e6, T / Tc, np.log10(p / pc)), axis=-1)


def to_xyz_pst(points, critical):
    """(T [K], s [J/(kg K)], p [Pa]) -> (delta_s/1e3, T/Tc, log10(p/pc))."""
    Tc, pc, sc = critical
    pts = np.asarray(points, dtype=float)

    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        return np.stack(((pts[..., 1] - sc) / 1e3,
                         pts[..., 0] / Tc,
                         np.log10(pts[..., 2] / pc)), axis=-1)


def to_xyz_hsp(points, critical):
    """(h [J/kg], s [J/kg/K], p [Pa]) -> (delta h [MJ/kg], delta s [kJ/kg/K], log p_r)."""
    hc, sc, pc = critical
    points = np.asarray(points, dtype=np.float64)

    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        return np.stack(((points[..., 0] - hc) / 1e6, (points[..., 1] - sc) / 1e3, np.log10(points[..., 2] / pc)), axis=-1)


def to_xyz_uvt(points, critical):
    """(T [K], v [m3/mol], u [J/kg]) -> (log v_r, T_r, delta u [MJ/kg])."""
    Tc, vc, uc = critical
    points = np.asarray(points, dtype=np.float64)

    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        return np.stack((np.log10(points[..., 1] / vc), points[..., 0] / Tc, (points[..., 2] - uc) / 1e6), axis=-1)


def to_xyz_gpt(points, critical, pressure_axis="log", p_window=None):
    """(T [K], p [Pa], g [J/kg]) -> (p-Achse (log10 p_r), T_r, delta g [MJ/kg]); critical: (Tc, pc, gc).

    p_window=(p_min, p_max): nur fuer die Log-Achse; Knoten ausserhalb des berechneten Druckfensters der
    Gleichgewichtsflaeche (z. B. homogene Zustaende bei p -> 0+) werden nicht dargestellt (NaN), die Daten bleiben
    unveraendert.
    """
    Tc, pc, gc = critical
    pts = np.asarray(points, dtype=np.float64)

    if pressure_axis not in PRESSURE_AXES:
        raise ValueError("pressure_axis muss eines von " + ", ".join(PRESSURE_AXES) + " sein")

    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore", over="ignore"):
        z = pressure_to_z(pts[..., 1], pc, pressure_axis)

        if p_window is not None and pressure_axis == "log":
            lo, hi = p_window
            z = np.where((pts[..., 1] >= lo * (1.0 - 1e-9)) & (pts[..., 1] <= hi * (1.0 + 1e-9)), z, np.nan)

        return np.stack((z, pts[..., 0] / Tc, (pts[..., 2] - gc) / 1e6), axis=-1)


def to_xyz_fvt(points, critical):
    """(T [K], v [m3/mol], f [J/kg]) -> (log v_r, T_r, delta f [MJ/kg]); critical: (Tc, vc, fc)."""
    Tc, vc, fc = critical
    pts = np.asarray(points, dtype=np.float64)

    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        return np.stack((np.log10(pts[..., 1] / vc), pts[..., 0] / Tc, (pts[..., 2] - fc) / 1e6), axis=-1)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Umkehrung (Darstellung -> native Werte)

def from_xyz(xyz, critical, pressure_axis="log"):
    """Umkehrung von to_xyz: -> (T, v, p)."""
    Tc, pc, vc = critical
    xyz = np.asarray(xyz, dtype=float)
    return np.stack((xyz[..., 1] * Tc, vc * 10.0 ** xyz[..., 0], z_to_pressure(xyz[..., 2], pc, pressure_axis)), axis=-1)


def from_xyz_pht(xyz, critical):
    """Umkehrung von to_xyz_pht: -> (T, h, p)."""
    Tc, pc, hc = critical
    xyz = np.asarray(xyz, dtype=float)
    return np.stack((xyz[..., 1] * Tc, hc + xyz[..., 0] * 1e6, pc * 10.0 ** xyz[..., 2]), axis=-1)


def from_xyz_pst(xyz, critical):
    """Umkehrung von to_xyz_pst: -> (T, s, p)."""
    Tc, pc, sc = critical
    xyz = np.asarray(xyz, dtype=float)
    return np.stack((xyz[..., 1] * Tc, sc + xyz[..., 0] * 1e3, pc * 10.0 ** xyz[..., 2]), axis=-1)


def from_xyz_hsp(xyz, critical):
    """Umkehrung von to_xyz_hsp: -> (h, s, p)."""
    hc, sc, pc = critical
    xyz = np.asarray(xyz, dtype=float)
    return np.stack((hc + xyz[..., 0] * 1e6, sc + xyz[..., 1] * 1e3, pc * 10.0 ** xyz[..., 2]), axis=-1)


def from_xyz_uvt(xyz, critical):
    """Umkehrung von to_xyz_uvt: -> (T, v, u)."""
    Tc, vc, uc = critical
    xyz = np.asarray(xyz, dtype=float)
    return np.stack((xyz[..., 1] * Tc, vc * 10.0 ** xyz[..., 0], uc + xyz[..., 2] * 1e6), axis=-1)


def from_xyz_gpt(xyz, critical, pressure_axis="log"):
    """Umkehrung von to_xyz_gpt: -> (T, p, g)."""
    Tc, pc, gc = critical
    xyz = np.asarray(xyz, dtype=float)
    return np.stack((xyz[..., 1] * Tc, z_to_pressure(xyz[..., 0], pc, pressure_axis), gc + xyz[..., 2] * 1e6), axis=-1)


def from_xyz_fvt(xyz, critical):
    """Umkehrung von to_xyz_fvt: -> (T, v, f)."""
    Tc, vc, fc = critical
    xyz = np.asarray(xyz, dtype=float)
    return np.stack((xyz[..., 1] * Tc, vc * 10.0 ** xyz[..., 0], fc + xyz[..., 2] * 1e6), axis=-1)


"""
Datei: diagram_registry.py
Projekt: ThermoSurface
Kategorie: Programmstruktur / Diagramm-Registry

Beschreibung:
Registriert die verfügbaren Diagrammtypen und ihre Darstellungseigenschaften.

Aufgaben:
- Hinterlegt Renderer-Namen, Layer und Standard-Sichtbarkeiten.
- Stellt Diagramm-Metadaten für GUI, Berechnung und Viewports bereit.

Abhängigkeiten:
- Projektmodule (direkt): visualization.py

Verwendet von:
- Projektmodule: calculation_manager.py, diagram_viewport.py, gui.py, main.py
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Callable, Optional

# Reihenfolge = Reihenfolge der Checkboxen in der GUI.
LAYER_LABELS = {
    "surface": "Zustandsoberfläche",
    "isothermen": "Isothermen – T = konstant",
    "isobaren": "Isobaren – p = konstant",
    "isochoren": "Isochoren – v = konstant",
    "binodale": "Siede- und Taulinie",
    "spinodale": "Spinodale",
    "zweiphasengebiet": "Zweiphasengebiet",
    "metastable_liquid_surface": "Metastabile Flüssigkeit (homogen)",
    "metastable_vapor_surface": "Metastabiler Dampf (homogen)",
    "unstable_surface": "Instabile Fortsetzung (HEOS)",
    "replacement_surface": "Ersatzfläche (Modell, keine EOS-Auswertung)",
    "critical_point": "Kritischer Punkt",
}

BASE_LAYERS = ("surface", "binodale", "zweiphasengebiet", "critical_point")
ISOLINE_LAYERS = ("isothermen", "isobaren", "isochoren")
ISOLINE_DEFINITIONS = {
    "isothermen": {"quantity": "T", "unit": "K", "label": LAYER_LABELS["isothermen"]},
    "isobaren": {"quantity": "p", "unit": "Pa", "label": LAYER_LABELS["isobaren"]},
    "isochoren": {"quantity": "v", "unit": "m³/mol", "label": LAYER_LABELS["isochoren"]},
}
PVT_LAYERS = tuple(LAYER_LABELS)
BASE_DEFAULT_ON = frozenset(BASE_LAYERS)
PVT_DEFAULT_OFF = frozenset({"spinodale", "zweiphasengebiet", "unstable_surface"})

# Schreibt ein Renderer-Ergebnis fuer die Statuszeile / Stoffinfo (reine Formatierung).
InfoFormatter = Callable[[dict, str], str]


def _info(fmt):
    def build(daten, stoff):
        meta = daten["meta"]
        return f"{stoff}: " + fmt(meta)
    return build


@dataclass
class DiagramSpec:
    id: str
    label: str
    render_name: str
    layers: tuple
    default_layers: frozenset
    confirm_label: str = ""
    info: Optional[InfoFormatter] = None
    hover: bool = False
    isolines: dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    # Von main.py angehaengt (Berechnung liegt in main.BERECHNUNGEN, siehe dort).
    cache_params: Optional[Callable] = field(default=None, repr=False)

    def render(self, plotter, daten):
        import visualization
        return getattr(visualization, self.render_name)(plotter, daten)

    def initial_layer_state(self):
        return {key: key in self.default_layers for key in LAYER_LABELS}

    def info_text(self, daten, stoff):
        return self.info(daten, stoff) if self.info else ""


class DiagramRegistry:
    def __init__(self):
        self._specs = {}

    def register(self, spec):
        if spec.id in self._specs:
            raise ValueError(f"Diagramm {spec.id!r} ist bereits registriert.")
        unknown = set(spec.layers) - set(LAYER_LABELS)
        if unknown:
            raise ValueError(f"Unbekannte Layer fuer {spec.id}: {sorted(unknown)}")
        if not spec.isolines:
            spec.isolines = {key: dict(ISOLINE_DEFINITIONS[key]) for key in spec.layers
                             if key in ISOLINE_DEFINITIONS}
        self._specs[spec.id] = spec
        return spec

    def attach(self, diagram_id, cache_params=None):
        spec = self.get(diagram_id)
        if cache_params is not None:
            spec.cache_params = cache_params

    def get(self, diagram_id):
        try:
            return self._specs[diagram_id]
        except KeyError:
            raise ValueError(f"Unbekannter Diagrammtyp: {diagram_id}") from None

    def ids(self):
        return list(self._specs)

    def __contains__(self, diagram_id):
        return diagram_id in self._specs

    def __iter__(self):
        return iter(self._specs.values())

    def __len__(self):
        return len(self._specs)


DIAGRAMS = DiagramRegistry()

DIAGRAMS.register(DiagramSpec(
    id="pvt", label="p-v-T (3D)", render_name="render_pvt", layers=PVT_LAYERS,
    default_layers=frozenset(set(PVT_LAYERS) - PVT_DEFAULT_OFF), confirm_label="p-v-T", hover=True))
DIAGRAMS.register(DiagramSpec(
    id="pht", label="p-h-T (3D)", render_name="render_pht", layers=BASE_LAYERS + ISOLINE_LAYERS,
    default_layers=BASE_DEFAULT_ON, confirm_label="p-h-T", hover=True,
    info=_info(lambda m: f"Tc={m['Tc']:.3f} K, pc={m['pc']/1e6:.3f} MPa, hc={m['hc']/1e3:.3f} kJ/kg")))
DIAGRAMS.register(DiagramSpec(
    id="pst", label="p-s-T (3D)", render_name="render_pst", layers=BASE_LAYERS + ISOLINE_LAYERS,
    default_layers=BASE_DEFAULT_ON, confirm_label="p-s-T", hover=True,
    info=_info(lambda m: f"Tc={m['Tc']:.3f} K, pc={m['pc']/1e6:.3f} MPa, sc={m['sc']/1e3:.3f} kJ/(kg K)")))
DIAGRAMS.register(DiagramSpec(
    id="hsp", label="h-s-p (3D)", render_name="render_hsp", layers=BASE_LAYERS + ISOLINE_LAYERS,
    default_layers=BASE_DEFAULT_ON, confirm_label="h-s-p", hover=True,
    info=_info(lambda m: (f"Tc={m['Tc']:.3f} K, pc={m['pc']/1e6:.3f} MPa, hc={m['hc']/1e3:.3f} kJ/kg, "
                          f"sc={m['sc']/1e3:.3f} kJ/(kg K)"))))
DIAGRAMS.register(DiagramSpec(
    id="uvt", label="u-v-T (3D)", render_name="render_uvt", layers=BASE_LAYERS + ISOLINE_LAYERS,
    default_layers=BASE_DEFAULT_ON, confirm_label="u-v-T", hover=True,
    info=_info(lambda m: f"Tc={m['Tc']:.3f} K, vc={m['vc']:.8g} m³/mol, uc={m['uc']/1e3:.3f} kJ/kg")))

# g-p-T / f-v-T: homogene Ebenen (metastabil/instabil) und Spinodalen sind optionale Ebenen und standardmaessig aus,
# damit die erste Ansicht die reine Gleichgewichtsflaeche zeigt. g-p-T hat keine Zweiphasenflaeche (g_l = g_g:
# das Zweiphasengebiet entartet zur Saettigungslinie, die als "Siede- und Taulinie" dargestellt wird).
HOMOGENEOUS_LAYERS = ("spinodale", "metastable_liquid_surface", "metastable_vapor_surface", "unstable_surface")
GPT_LAYERS = tuple(k for k in BASE_LAYERS if k != "zweiphasengebiet") + ISOLINE_LAYERS + HOMOGENEOUS_LAYERS
FVT_LAYERS = BASE_LAYERS + ISOLINE_LAYERS + HOMOGENEOUS_LAYERS


def _gpt_info(daten, stoff):
    meta = daten["meta"]
    text = f"{stoff}: Tc={meta['Tc']:.3f} K, pc={meta['pc']/1e6:.3f} MPa, gc={meta['gc']/1e3:.3f} kJ/kg"
    notice = daten.get("pressure_axis_notice")
    return f"{text}. {notice}" if notice else text


DIAGRAMS.register(DiagramSpec(
    id="gpt", label="g-p-T (3D)", render_name="render_gpt", layers=GPT_LAYERS,
    default_layers=frozenset({"surface", "binodale", "critical_point"}), confirm_label="g-p-T", info=_gpt_info,
    hover=True))
DIAGRAMS.register(DiagramSpec(
    id="fvt", label="f-v-T (3D)", render_name="render_fvt", layers=FVT_LAYERS,
    default_layers=BASE_DEFAULT_ON, confirm_label="f-v-T", hover=True,
    info=_info(lambda m: f"Tc={m['Tc']:.3f} K, vc={m['vc']:.8g} m³/mol, fc={m['fc']/1e3:.3f} kJ/kg")))

"""
Datei: diagram_viewport.py
Projekt: ThermoSurface
Kategorie: GUI / 3D-Viewport

Beschreibung:
Verwaltet eine interaktive PyVista-Ansicht für genau einen Diagrammtyp.

Aufgaben:
- Steuert Rendering, Kamera, Layer, Hover und Punktauswahl.
- Wendet manuelle Viewportgrenzen und geometrisches Clipping an.

Abhängigkeiten:
- Projektmodule (direkt): axes_view.py, diagram_axes.py, diagram_coords.py,
  diagram_registry.py, fvt_tangent.py, geometry_clip.py, visualization.py
- Weitere Imports: viewport_bounds.py, viewport_clipping.py, viewport_recovery.py
- Bibliotheken: PySide6, numpy, pyvista, pyvistaqt

Verwendet von:
- Projektmodule: diagram_workspace.py
"""

import logging
import math
import time

import numpy as np
from PySide6.QtCore import QEvent, QObject, QTimer, Qt, Signal
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication, QFrame, QLabel, QVBoxLayout
from pyvistaqt import QtInteractor

import axes_view
import diagram_coords
import geometry_clip
from diagram_axes import ViewportBoundsError, pressure_axis_of
from diagram_registry import DIAGRAMS, LAYER_LABELS
from viewport_bounds import ViewportState
from viewport_clipping import ClipRegistry
from viewport_recovery import DIRTY, DISPOSED, EMPTY, FAILED, READY, RENDERING
from visualization import pick_hover_text

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("thermosurface.viewport")

GRID_ACTOR_KEYS = ("surface", "surface_single_phase", "metastable_liquid_surface", "metastable_vapor_surface",
                   "unstable_surface", "replacement_surface")


class _Header(QLabel):
    clicked = Signal()
    double_clicked = Signal()

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        self.clicked.emit()
        super().mousePressEvent(event)

    def mouseDoubleClickEvent(self, event):
        self.double_clicked.emit()
        super().mouseDoubleClickEvent(event)


class _MouseFilter(QObject):
    """Meldet Doppelklicks und Einfachklicks (ohne Ziehen) auf den Interactor, ohne Ereignisse zu verbrauchen.

    Rotation (Ziehen) und Doppelklick-Maximierung bleiben unberuehrt; ein Einfachklick wird erst gemeldet,
    wenn kein Doppelklick folgt.
    """

    double_clicked = Signal()
    clicked = Signal(int, int)     # Qt-Widgetkoordinaten

    DRAG_TOLERANCE_PX = 4

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.click_enabled = False
        self._press = None
        self._pending = None
        self._after_double = False
        self._timer = QTimer(self)
        self._timer.setSingleShot(True)
        self._timer.timeout.connect(self._emit_click)

    def _emit_click(self):
        if self._pending is not None:
            x, y = self._pending
            self._pending = None
            self.clicked.emit(x, y)

    def eventFilter(self, obj, event):
        kind = event.type()
        if kind == QEvent.Type.MouseButtonDblClick and event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            self._timer.stop()
            self._pending = None
            self._after_double = True
            self.double_clicked.emit()
        elif kind == QEvent.Type.MouseButtonPress and event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            self._press = (event.position().x(), event.position().y())
            self._after_double = False
        elif kind == QEvent.Type.MouseButtonRelease and event.button() == Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton:
            press, self._press = self._press, None
            if self.click_enabled and press is not None and not self._after_double:
                dx, dy = event.position().x() - press[0], event.position().y() - press[1]
                if dx * dx + dy * dy <= self.DRAG_TOLERANCE_PX ** 2:
                    self._pending = (int(event.position().x()), int(event.position().y()))
                    self._timer.start(QApplication.doubleClickInterval())
        return False


class DiagramViewport(QFrame):
    activated = Signal(object)
    maximize_requested = Signal(object)
    status_message = Signal(str)
    point_clicked = Signal(object, int, int)   # (viewport, x, y) in VTK-Displaykoordinaten

    def __init__(self, diagram_id, parent=None, show_header=True):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.spec = DIAGRAMS.get(diagram_id)
        self.diagram_id = diagram_id
        self.daten = None
        self.actors = {}
        self.result_key = None
        self.layer_state = self.spec.initial_layer_state()
        self.grid_on = True
        self.render_count = 0
        self.info_text = ""
        self.tooltips = {}
        self._hover_installed = False
        self._hover_observer_id = None
        self._last_hover_text = None
        self._last_hover_at = 0.0
        self._axes_observer_installed = False
        self._axes_observer_id = None
        self._disposed = False
        self._render_state = EMPTY
        self.view_state = ViewportState(diagram_id)
        self.clip_registry = ClipRegistry()
        self.default_camera = None
        self._manual_box = None

        self.setObjectName("diagramViewport")
        self.setFrameShape(QFrame.Shape.NoFrame)
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(2, 2, 2, 2)
        layout.setSpacing(2)

        self.header = _Header(self.spec.label)
        self.header.setAlignment(Qt.AlignmentFlag.AlignCenter)
        self.header.setCursor(Qt.CursorShape.PointingHandCursor)
        self.header.setToolTip("Klick: Diagramm aktivieren (Layersteuerung) – Doppelklick: maximieren")
        self.header.clicked.connect(lambda: self.activated.emit(self))
        self.header.double_clicked.connect(lambda: self.maximize_requested.emit(self))
        layout.addWidget(self.header)
        self.header.setVisible(show_header)

        self.plotter = QtInteractor(self)
        layout.addWidget(self.plotter, 1)
        self.plotter.add_camera_orientation_widget(animate=True, n_frames=20)

        self._filter = _MouseFilter(self)
        self._filter.double_clicked.connect(lambda: self.maximize_requested.emit(self))
        self._filter.clicked.connect(self._on_qt_click)
        self.plotter.installEventFilter(self._filter)
        self._markers = []          # [(selection_index, display_xyz, color)]
        self._projected_markers = []
        self._marker_actors = []
        self._tangent_data = None
        self._tangent_show_slice = False
        self._tangent_actors = []
        self.set_active(False)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Zustand
    @property
    def has_result(self):
        return self.daten is not None

    def set_header_visible(self, visible):
        self.header.setVisible(bool(visible))

    def set_active(self, active):
        color = "#2d7ff9" if active else "#8a8a8a"
        width = 2 if active else 1
        self.setStyleSheet(f"#diagramViewport {{ border: {width}px solid {color}; }}")
        self.header.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold;" + ("color: #2d7ff9;" if active else ""))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Rendern
    def show_result(self, daten, key=None):
        """Rendert `daten` mit dem registrierten Renderer in genau diesen Plotter."""
        if self._disposed:
            raise RuntimeError("Ein freigegebener Viewport kann nicht neu gerendert werden.")
        self._render_state = RENDERING
        LOGGER.debug("event=render_begin diagram=%s viewport=%x key=%s",
                     self.diagram_id, id(self), key)
        try:
            self.actors = self.spec.render(self.plotter, daten)
            self.daten = daten
            self.result_key = key
            self.render_count += 1
            self._manual_box = None
            if self.spec.hover:
                self._install_hover()
            self._install_axes_observer()
            self.clip_registry.register(self.actors)
            self.apply_layers(render=False)
            self.apply_grid(render=False)
            default_bounds = axes_view.auto_bounds(self.diagram_id, self.actors)
            if default_bounds is not None:
                axes_view.set_default_view(self.plotter, default_bounds)
                self.update_axes(render=False)
            self.default_camera = self.camera_state()
            self._marker_actors = []
            self._tangent_actors = []
            self._apply_view(fit_camera=self.view_state.enabled, render=False)
            recovery_camera = getattr(self, "_recovery_camera", None)
            if recovery_camera is not None:
                self.plotter.camera_position = recovery_camera
                self.update_axes(render=False)
                del self._recovery_camera
            if not self._markers:
                self.plotter.render()
            if not self.scene_is_ready():
                raise RuntimeError("Renderer lieferte keine gueltige Zustandsoberflaeche.")
        except Exception:
            self._render_state = FAILED
            LOGGER.exception("event=render_error diagram=%s viewport=%x key=%s",
                             self.diagram_id, id(self), key)
            raise
        self._render_state = READY
        LOGGER.debug("event=render_end diagram=%s viewport=%x actors=%d",
                     self.diagram_id, id(self), self._scene_actor_count())

    @property
    def render_state(self):
        if self._disposed:
            return DISPOSED
        if self._render_state == READY and not self.scene_is_ready():
            return FAILED
        return self._render_state

    def mark_scene_dirty(self):
        if not self._disposed:
            self._render_state = DIRTY

    def _surface_actors(self):
        surfaces = self.actors.get("surface")
        return list(surfaces) if isinstance(surfaces, (list, tuple)) else [surfaces]

    def _scene_actor_count(self):
        return sum(actor is not None for actor in self._surface_actors())

    def scene_is_ready(self):
        if (self._disposed or self.daten is None or self.plotter._closed
                or self.plotter.iren is None or self.plotter.renderer.GetRenderWindow() is None):
            return False
        valid_surface = False
        for actor in self._surface_actors():
            if actor is None:
                continue
            try:
                if not self.plotter.renderer.HasViewProp(actor):
                    return False
                mapper = actor.GetMapper()
                dataset = mapper.GetInput() if mapper is not None else None
                if dataset is None:
                    return False
                if self.clip_registry.is_clipped(actor):
                    dataset = self.clip_registry.original_dataset(actor)
                bounds = dataset.GetBounds() if dataset is not None else None
                if (dataset is not None and dataset.GetNumberOfCells() > 0 and bounds is not None
                        and all(math.isfinite(value) for value in bounds)):
                    valid_surface = True
            except (AttributeError, RuntimeError, TypeError):
                LOGGER.debug("Oberflaechengeometrie fuer %s nicht verfuegbar", self.diagram_id, exc_info=True)
                return False
        return valid_surface

    def _install_axes_observer(self):
        """Tick-/Titelkanten folgen der Kamera: nach jeder Interaktion neu zeichnen (nur Beschriftung, keine Kamera)."""
        if self._axes_observer_installed:
            return

        def on_end(_obj, _event):
            try:
                if self.daten is not None and not self._disposed:
                    self.update_axes(render=True)
            except Exception:
                LOGGER.debug("Achsenaktualisierung nach Interaktion fehlgeschlagen", exc_info=True)

        self._axes_observer_id = self.plotter.iren.add_observer("EndInteractionEvent", on_end)
        self._axes_observer_installed = True

    def _install_hover(self):
        if self._hover_installed:
            return

        def on_move(_obj, _event):
            now = time.monotonic()
            if now - self._last_hover_at < 1 / 30:
                return
            self._last_hover_at = now
            try:
                x, y = self.plotter.iren.get_event_position()
                text = pick_hover_text(self.plotter, x, y)
            except Exception:
                return
            if text != self._last_hover_text:
                self._last_hover_text = text
                self.status_message.emit(text or "")

        self._hover_observer_id = self.plotter.iren.add_observer("MouseMoveEvent", on_move)
        self._hover_installed = True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Layer
    def layer_available(self, key):
        if key not in self.spec.layers:
            return False
        objects = self.actors.get(key)
        if isinstance(objects, (list, tuple)):
            return any(actor is not None for actor in objects)
        return objects is not None

    def available_layers(self):
        return {key: self.layer_available(key) for key in LAYER_LABELS}

    def effective_layers(self):
        return {key: bool(self.layer_state.get(key, False)) and self.layer_available(key) for key in LAYER_LABELS}

    def set_layer_state(self, state):
        for key, value in state.items():
            if key in self.layer_state:
                self.layer_state[key] = bool(value)

    def _set_visibility(self, key, visible):
        objects = self.actors.get(key)
        if objects is None:
            return
        for actor in (objects if isinstance(objects, (list, tuple)) else [objects]):
            if actor is not None:
                actor.visibility = bool(visible)

    def apply_layers(self, render=True):
        if not self.actors:
            return
        effective = self.effective_layers()
        self._set_visibility("surface", effective["surface"])
        self._set_visibility("zweiphasengebiet", effective["zweiphasengebiet"])
        if self.diagram_id == "pvt":
            # Die randkonforme Einphasenflaeche bleibt unabhaengig sichtbar (nur 'surface' steuert sie).
            self._set_visibility("surface_single_phase", False)
        for key, visible in effective.items():
            if key not in ("surface", "zweiphasengebiet"):
                self._set_visibility(key, visible)
        # Zentrale Achsenaktualisierung: Layerwechsel aendert weder Kamera noch manuelle Grenzen.
        self.update_axes(render=False)
        if render:
            self.plotter.render()
