    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Ansichtsbereich (TS-VP04..06)
    def update_axes(self, render=False):
        """Achsenrahmen, physikalische Ticks und Titel; manuelle Box hat Vorrang vor automatischen Grenzen."""
        if not self.actors or self.daten is None:
            return
        axes_view.apply_axes(self.plotter, self.diagram_id, self.daten, self.actors, box=self._manual_box)
        if render:
            self.plotter.render()

    def _new_point_values(self, xyz):
        """Punktdaten neuer Schnittpunkte aus den Darstellungskoordinaten (Rueckwaertstransformation, keine HEOS)."""
        if self.diagram_id != "pvt":
            return None
        T, v, p = diagram_coords.from_xyz(xyz, self.daten["critical_point"], pressure_axis_of(self.daten)).T
        return {"T": T, "v": v, "p_raw": p}

    def _apply_view(self, fit_camera=False, render=True):
        """Clippt die Originalgeometrie an die manuelle Box (oder stellt sie wieder her) und aktualisiert Achsen.

        Rein darstellungsbezogen: Berechnungsarrays, Ergebnis-Dictionary und Cache bleiben unberuehrt.
        """
        if not self.actors or self.daten is None:
            return None
        try:
            box = self.view_state.box(self.daten)
        except ViewportBoundsError as exc:
            LOGGER.warning("Gespeicherte Grenzen nicht anwendbar: %s", exc)
            self.status_message.emit(f"Ansichtsbereich nicht anwendbar, automatische Ansicht: {exc}")
            self.view_state.reset()
            box = None
        stats = self.clip_registry.apply(box, self._new_point_values)
        self._manual_box = box
        if fit_camera:
            target = box if box is not None else axes_view.auto_bounds(self.diagram_id, self.actors)
            if target is not None:
                axes_view.fit_camera(self.plotter, target)
        self.update_axes(render=False)
        if box is not None and stats["empty"] == stats["actors"]:
            self.status_message.emit("Ansichtsbereich enthält keine Geometrie (Rahmen und Achsen bleiben sichtbar).")
        if self._markers or self._projected_markers:
            self._draw_markers()
        elif render:
            self.plotter.render()
        return stats

    def apply_manual_view(self, texts):
        """Prueft die sechs Grenzen (Anzeigeeinheiten) und wendet sie an; bei Fehler bleibt alles unveraendert.

        Wirft ViewportBoundsError. Die Kamera wird auf die neue Box eingepasst (Blickrichtung bleibt erhalten).
        """
        if self.daten is None:
            raise ViewportBoundsError("Noch kein Ergebnis in diesem Diagramm: zuerst die Berechnung starten.")
        candidate = self.view_state.copy()
        candidate.apply_fields(texts, self.daten)
        previous, self.view_state = self.view_state, candidate
        try:
            stats = self._apply_view(fit_camera=True, render=True)
        except Exception as exc:
            LOGGER.exception("Ansichtsbereich konnte nicht dargestellt werden")
            self.view_state = previous                       # letzter gueltiger Ausschnitt bleibt erhalten
            self._apply_view(fit_camera=False, render=True)
            raise ViewportBoundsError(f"Darstellung des Ausschnitts fehlgeschlagen: {exc}") from exc
        self.view_state.camera_state = self.camera_state()
        return stats

    def reset_manual_view(self):
        """Manuelle Begrenzung aus: vollstaendige Originalgeometrie, automatische Grenzen, Kamera auf alles."""
        self.view_state.reset()
        return self._apply_view(fit_camera=True, render=True)

    def set_view_state(self, state, fit_camera=False):
        """Uebernimmt einen gespeicherten Zustand (z. B. nach Neuerzeugung des Viewports)."""
        self.view_state = state
        if self.daten is not None:
            self._apply_view(fit_camera=fit_camera, render=True)

    def restore_default_camera(self):
        """Stellt die Standardkamera des Renderers wieder her (unabhaengig vom Darstellungsfenster)."""
        if self.default_camera is not None:
            self.restore_camera(self.default_camera)
            self.update_axes(render=True)

    def manual_box(self):
        return self._manual_box

    def point_in_manual_view(self, xyz):
        return self._manual_box is None or bool(geometry_clip.points_in_box([xyz], self._manual_box)[0])

    def point_in_visible_surface_bounds(self, xyz):
        bounds = axes_view.visible_bounds({key: self.actors.get(key) for key in ("surface", "zweiphasengebiet")})
        return bounds is not None and bool(geometry_clip.points_in_box([xyz], bounds)[0])

    def clip_stats(self):
        return {"enabled": self._manual_box is not None, "box": self._manual_box,
                "actors": len(self.clip_registry.entries)}

    def apply_grid(self, render=True):
        if not self.actors:
            return
        for key in GRID_ACTOR_KEYS:
            objects = self.actors.get(key)
            if objects is None:
                continue
            for actor in (objects if isinstance(objects, (list, tuple)) else [objects]):
                if actor is not None:
                    actor.prop.show_edges = bool(self.grid_on)
        if render:
            self.plotter.render()

    def set_grid(self, on):
        self.grid_on = bool(on)
        self.apply_grid()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Punktauswahl (Zustandsvergleich)
    @property
    def picking_enabled(self):
        return self._filter.click_enabled

    @picking_enabled.setter
    def picking_enabled(self, enabled):
        self._filter.click_enabled = bool(enabled)

    def _on_qt_click(self, x, y):
        ratio = self.plotter.devicePixelRatioF()
        self.point_clicked.emit(self, int(round(x * ratio)), int(round((self.plotter.height() - 1 - y) * ratio)))

    def set_selection_markers(self, markers, projected_markers=None):
        """Aktualisiert Quellen- und diagrammübergreifende Auswahlmarker ohne Szenen-Neuaufbau."""
        self._markers = [(int(i), tuple(float(c) for c in xyz), color) for i, xyz, color in markers if xyz is not None]
        if projected_markers is not None:
            self._projected_markers = [(int(i), tuple(float(c) for c in xyz), color)
                                       for i, xyz, color in projected_markers if xyz is not None]
        self._draw_markers()

    def _draw_markers(self):
        import pyvista as pv
        for actor in self._marker_actors:
            try:
                self.plotter.remove_actor(actor, render=False)
            except Exception:
                LOGGER.debug("Markierung konnte nicht entfernt werden", exc_info=True)
        self._marker_actors = []
        markers_by_index = {index: (index, xyz, color) for index, xyz, color in self._projected_markers}
        markers_by_index.update({index: (index, xyz, color) for index, xyz, color in self._markers})
        for index, xyz, color in markers_by_index.values():
            # Marker ausserhalb des manuellen Ansichtsbereichs werden ausgeblendet, aber nicht aus den Daten geloescht.
            if not self.point_in_manual_view(xyz):
                continue
            point = pv.PolyData([xyz])
            self._marker_actors.append(self.plotter.add_mesh(
                point, color=color, point_size=16, render_points_as_spheres=True, pickable=False, reset_camera=False))
            self._marker_actors.append(self.plotter.add_point_labels(
                [xyz], [str(index)], font_size=16, bold=True, text_color="black", show_points=False, shape=None,
                always_visible=True, pickable=False, reset_camera=False, render=False))
        self.plotter.render()

    @property
    def marker_count(self):
        return len(self._markers)

    @property
    def projected_marker_count(self):
        return len(self._projected_markers)

    def set_fvt_tangent(self, tangent, show_slice=False):
        """Aktualisiert die nicht-pickbare gemeinsame Tangente, ohne die Szene oder Kamera zurueckzusetzen."""
        self._tangent_data = tangent
        self._tangent_show_slice = bool(show_slice)
        if self.diagram_id != "fvt" or not self.has_result or self._disposed:
            return
        import pyvista as pv

        for actor in self._tangent_actors:
            try:
                self.plotter.remove_actor(actor, render=False)
            except (RuntimeError, ValueError):
                LOGGER.debug("Tangenten-Overlay konnte nicht entfernt werden", exc_info=True)
        self._tangent_actors = []
        if tangent is not None:
            from fvt_tangent import tangent_display_points

            xyz = tangent_display_points(tangent, self.daten)
            line = pv.lines_from_points(xyz)
            self._tangent_actors.append(self.plotter.add_mesh(
                line, color="lime", line_width=5, pickable=False, reset_camera=False, render=False))
            endpoints = pv.PolyData(xyz[[0, -1]])
            self._tangent_actors.append(self.plotter.add_mesh(
                endpoints, color="lime", point_size=18, render_points_as_spheres=True, pickable=False,
                reset_camera=False, render=False))
            if self._tangent_show_slice:
                self._add_tangent_slice_plane(pv, tangent.temperature)
        self.plotter.render()

    def _add_tangent_slice_plane(self, pv, temperature):
        bounds = self.plotter.bounds
        if bounds is None or len(bounds) != 6 or not all(math.isfinite(value) for value in bounds):
            return
        y = float(temperature) / float(self.daten["critical_point"][0])
        points = np.array(((bounds[0], y, bounds[4]), (bounds[1], y, bounds[4]),
                           (bounds[1], y, bounds[5]), (bounds[0], y, bounds[5])))
        plane = pv.PolyData(points, np.array((4, 0, 1, 2, 3)))
        self._tangent_actors.append(self.plotter.add_mesh(
            plane, color="lime", opacity=0.12, show_edges=False, pickable=False, reset_camera=False, render=False))

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Kamera
    def camera_state(self):
        return [tuple(float(x) for x in point) for point in self.plotter.camera_position]

    def restore_camera(self, state):
        self.plotter.camera_position = state
        self.plotter.render()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Lebenszyklus
    def dispose(self):
        """Gibt Plotter, Beobachter und Qt-Widget frei (idempotent)."""
        if self._disposed:
            return
        LOGGER.debug("event=viewport_dispose diagram=%s viewport=%x actors=%d",
                     self.diagram_id, id(self), self._scene_actor_count())
        self._disposed = True
        self._render_state = DISPOSED
        try:
            self.plotter.removeEventFilter(self._filter)
            for observer_id in (self._hover_observer_id, self._axes_observer_id):
                if observer_id is not None:
                    self.plotter.iren.remove_observer(observer_id)
            self.plotter.clear()
            self.plotter.close()
        except Exception:
            LOGGER.exception("Viewport %s konnte nicht sauber geschlossen werden", self.diagram_id)
        self.actors = {}
        self.daten = None
        self.hide()
        self.setParent(None)
        self.deleteLater()

"""
Datei: diagram_workspace.py
Projekt: ThermoSurface
Kategorie: GUI / Mehrfachansicht

Beschreibung:
Ordnet mehrere DiagramViewport-Instanzen in einer Qt-Kachelansicht an.

Aufgaben:
- Erstellt, aktiviert, maximiert und entfernt Diagramm-Viewports.
- Erhält Ansichtseinstellungen bei gezieltem Austausch eines Viewports.

Abhängigkeiten:
- Projektmodule (direkt): diagram_viewport.py
- Weitere Imports: tile_layout.py
- Bibliotheken: PySide6

Verwendet von:
- Projektmodule: gui.py
"""

import logging
import math

from PySide6.QtCore import Signal
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QGridLayout, QHBoxLayout, QPushButton, QVBoxLayout, QWidget

from diagram_viewport import DiagramViewport
from tile_layout import tile_grid_shape

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("thermosurface.workspace")


class DiagramWorkspace(QWidget):
    active_changed = Signal(object)      # DiagramViewport | None
    maximized_changed = Signal(object)   # DiagramViewport | None
    status_message = Signal(str)
    point_clicked = Signal(object, int, int)   # (viewport, x, y): Einfachklick fuer die Punktauswahl

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.viewports = {}
        self._active = None
        self._maximized = None
        self._single_mode = True
        self._grid_extent = (0, 0)
        self._picking = False

        root = QVBoxLayout(self)
        root.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        root.setSpacing(2)
        bar = QHBoxLayout()
        bar.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.return_button = QPushButton("◀ Zurück zur Mehrfachansicht")
        self.return_button.setVisible(False)
        self.return_button.clicked.connect(self.restore)
        bar.addWidget(self.return_button)
        bar.addStretch(1)
        root.addLayout(bar)

        self._grid_host = QWidget()
        self._grid = QGridLayout(self._grid_host)
        self._grid.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self._grid.setSpacing(4)
        root.addWidget(self._grid_host, 1)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Zugriff
    @property
    def active(self):
        return self._active

    @property
    def maximized(self):
        return self._maximized

    def ordered(self):
        return list(self.viewports.values())

    def set_single_mode(self, single):
        self._single_mode = bool(single)
        for viewport in self.viewports.values():
            viewport.set_header_visible(not self._single_mode)

    @property
    def picking_enabled(self):
        return self._picking

    @picking_enabled.setter
    def picking_enabled(self, enabled):
        self._picking = bool(enabled)
        for viewport in self.viewports.values():
            viewport.picking_enabled = self._picking

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Aufbau
    def ensure(self, diagram_ids):
        """Haelt genau die Viewports fuer `diagram_ids` (Reihenfolge = Kachelreihenfolge) vor.

        Vorhandene Viewports gleicher ID bleiben erhalten (Kamera, Layer); nicht mehr
        benoetigte werden freigegeben, fehlende neu erzeugt. Gibt (viewports, neu) zurueck.
        """
        diagram_ids = list(dict.fromkeys(diagram_ids))
        previous = self._active
        if self._maximized is not None:
            self.restore()

        for diagram_id in list(self.viewports):
            if diagram_id not in diagram_ids:
                self._remove(diagram_id)

        created = set()
        ordered = {}
        for diagram_id in diagram_ids:
            viewport = self.viewports.get(diagram_id)
            if viewport is None:
                viewport = self._new_viewport(diagram_id)
                created.add(diagram_id)
            ordered[diagram_id] = viewport
        self.viewports = ordered

        if self._active is None:
            self._active = next(iter(self.viewports.values()), None)
            if self._active is not None:
                self._active.set_active(True)
        if self._active is not previous:
            self.active_changed.emit(self._active)
        self._relayout()
        return ordered, created

    def _new_viewport(self, diagram_id):
        viewport = DiagramViewport(diagram_id, self._grid_host, show_header=not self._single_mode)
        viewport.activated.connect(self.set_active)
        viewport.maximize_requested.connect(self.maximize)
        viewport.status_message.connect(self.status_message)
        viewport.point_clicked.connect(self.point_clicked)
        viewport.picking_enabled = self._picking
        return viewport

    def replace_viewport(self, diagram_id):
        """Replace one renderer while preserving its diagram-local presentation preferences."""
        old = self.viewports.get(diagram_id)
        if old is None:
            return None

        was_active = old is self._active
        was_maximized = old is self._maximized
        replacement = self._new_viewport(diagram_id)
        replacement.layer_state = dict(old.layer_state)
        replacement.grid_on = old.grid_on
        replacement.tooltips = dict(old.tooltips)
        replacement.view_state = old.view_state.copy()
        replacement._markers = list(old._markers)
        try:
            camera = old.camera_state()
            if len(camera) != 3 or not all(len(point) == 3 for point in camera):
                raise ValueError("ungueltige Kamerakoordinaten")
            if not all(math.isfinite(value) for point in camera for value in point):
                raise ValueError("nicht endliche Kamerakoordinaten")
            replacement._recovery_camera = camera
        except (AttributeError, RuntimeError, TypeError, ValueError):
            LOGGER.warning("Kamerazustand fuer %s nicht uebernehmbar; Standardkamera wird verwendet.",
                           diagram_id, exc_info=True)
        self._grid.removeWidget(old)
        self.viewports[diagram_id] = replacement
        if was_active:
            self._active = replacement
        if was_maximized:
            self._maximized = replacement
        old.dispose()
        replacement.set_active(was_active)
        self._relayout()
        if was_active:
            self.active_changed.emit(replacement)
        if was_maximized:
            self.maximized_changed.emit(replacement)
        return replacement

    def _remove(self, diagram_id):
        viewport = self.viewports.pop(diagram_id)
        self._grid.removeWidget(viewport)
        if viewport is self._active:
            self._active = None
        viewport.dispose()

    def clear(self):
        """Ansicht zuruecksetzen (Moduswechsel); Berechnungsdaten liegen im Cache, nicht hier."""
        previous = self._active
        if self._maximized is not None:
            self.restore()
        for diagram_id in list(self.viewports):
            self._remove(diagram_id)
        self._active = None
        if previous is not None:
            self.active_changed.emit(None)
        self._relayout()

    def close_all(self):
        self.clear()

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Aktivierung
    def set_active(self, viewport):
        self._set_active(viewport)

    def _set_active(self, viewport):
        if viewport is self._active and (viewport is None or viewport in self.viewports.values()):
            if viewport is not None:
                viewport.set_active(True)
            return
        if self._active is not None and self._active in self.viewports.values():
            self._active.set_active(False)
        self._active = viewport
        if viewport is not None:
            viewport.set_active(True)
        self.active_changed.emit(viewport)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Maximieren
    def maximize(self, viewport):
        """Zeigt nur diesen bereits vorhandenen Viewport; es wird nichts berechnet."""
        if viewport is None or viewport not in self.viewports.values():
            return
        if self._single_mode or len(self.viewports) < 2 or self._maximized is not None:
            return
        self._maximized = viewport
        self._set_active(viewport)
        self.return_button.setVisible(True)
        self._relayout()
        self.maximized_changed.emit(viewport)

    def restore(self):
        if self._maximized is None:
            return
        self._maximized = None
        self.return_button.setVisible(False)
        self._relayout()
        self.maximized_changed.emit(None)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Layout
    def _relayout(self):
        for viewport in self.viewports.values():
            self._grid.removeWidget(viewport)

        old_rows, old_cols = self._grid_extent
        for row in range(old_rows):
            self._grid.setRowStretch(row, 0)
        for col in range(old_cols):
            self._grid.setColumnStretch(col, 0)

        if self._maximized is not None:
            for viewport in self.viewports.values():
                viewport.setVisible(viewport is self._maximized)
            self._grid.addWidget(self._maximized, 0, 0)
            self._grid.setRowStretch(0, 1)
            self._grid.setColumnStretch(0, 1)
            self._grid_extent = (1, 1)
            return

        rows, cols = tile_grid_shape(len(self.viewports))
        for index, viewport in enumerate(self.viewports.values()):
            self._grid.addWidget(viewport, index // cols, index % cols)
            viewport.setVisible(True)
        # Gleichmaessige Kacheln; nicht belegte Kacheln bleiben leer (kein Renderer).
        for row in range(rows):
            self._grid.setRowStretch(row, 1)
        for col in range(cols):
            self._grid.setColumnStretch(col, 1)
        self._grid_extent = (rows, cols)


"""
Datei: fvt_slice_view.py
Projekt: ThermoSurface
Kategorie: GUI / f-v-Isotherme

Beschreibung:
Zeigt die gemeinsame f-v-T-Tangente in einer ergänzenden 2D-Matplotlib-Ansicht.

Aufgaben:
- Zeichnet Tangente, Sättigungspunkte und homogene Isothermenabschnitte.
- Berechnet zusätzliche Kurvendaten asynchron über einen Einzel-Worker.

Abhängigkeiten:
- Projektmodule (direkt): fvt_tangent.py
- Bibliotheken: PySide6, matplotlib, numpy

Verwendet von:
- Projektmodule: gui.py
"""

from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
import logging

import numpy as np
from matplotlib.backends.backend_qtagg import FigureCanvasQTAgg, NavigationToolbar2QT
from matplotlib.figure import Figure
from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, Signal, Slot
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QVBoxLayout, QWidget

from fvt_tangent import tangent_plot_data

LOGGER = logging.getLogger("thermosurface.fvt_slice")


class FvtIsothermSampler(QObject):
    completed = Signal(object, object, object)
    _finished = Signal(int, object, object, object)

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self._executor = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1, thread_name_prefix="fvt-isotherm")
        self._generation = 0
        self._running = False
        self._pending = None
        self._closed = False
        self._finished.connect(self._on_finished)

    def invalidate(self):
        self._generation += 1
        self._pending = None

    def request(self, tangent, data):
        self.invalidate()
        self._pending = (self._generation, tangent, {"meta": dict(data["meta"])})
        if not self._running:
            self._start_pending()

    def _start_pending(self):
        generation, tangent, data = self._pending
        self._pending = None
        self._running = True

        def sample():
            from fvt_tangent import homogeneous_fvt_isotherm
            return homogeneous_fvt_isotherm(tangent, data)

        def finished(future):
            try:
                branches = future.result()
            except Exception as exc:
                LOGGER.exception("Homogene f-v-Isotherme konnte nicht berechnet werden")
                self._finished.emit(generation, tangent, None, str(exc) or type(exc).__name__)
            else:
                self._finished.emit(generation, tangent, branches, None)

        self._executor.submit(sample).add_done_callback(finished)

    @Slot(int, object, object, object)
    def _on_finished(self, generation, tangent, branches, error):
        self._running = False
        if not self._closed and generation == self._generation:
            self.completed.emit(tangent, branches, error)
        if not self._closed and self._pending is not None:
            self._start_pending()

    def shutdown(self):
        self._closed = True
        self.invalidate()
        self._executor.shutdown(wait=True, cancel_futures=True)


class FvtSliceView(QWidget):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.figure = Figure()
        self.canvas = FigureCanvasQTAgg(self.figure)
        self.toolbar = NavigationToolbar2QT(self.canvas, self)
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(2, 2, 2, 2)
        layout.addWidget(self.toolbar)
        layout.addWidget(self.canvas)

    def show_error(self, message):
        self.figure.clear()
        axes = self.figure.add_subplot(111)
        axes.text(0.5, 0.5, message, ha="center", va="center", wrap=True, transform=axes.transAxes)
        axes.set_axis_off()
        self.toolbar.update()
        self.canvas.draw_idle()

    def update_tangent(self, tangent, homogeneous=None):
        self.figure.clear()
        axes = self.figure.add_subplot(111)
        data = tangent_plot_data(tangent)
        axes.plot(data["volume"], data["helmholtz"], color="green", linewidth=2.2, label="Gemeinsame Tangente")
        axes.plot((0.0, tangent.v_l),
                  (tangent.mu, tangent.mu - tangent.p_sat * tangent.v_l),
                  color="green", linewidth=1.2, linestyle="--")
        axes.scatter((tangent.v_l, tangent.v_v), (tangent.f_l_molar, tangent.f_v_molar),
                     color=("blue", "red"), zorder=3)
        axes.scatter((data["intercept_volume"],), (data["intercept"],), color="black", marker="x", zorder=4)
        axes.annotate("μ = gₘ", (0.0, data["intercept"]), xytext=(6, 8), textcoords="offset points")
        if homogeneous is not None:
            for index, (volumes, energies) in enumerate(homogeneous):
                axes.plot(np.asarray(volumes), np.asarray(energies), color="gray", alpha=0.8,
                          label="Homogene HEOS-Fortsetzung" if index == 0 else None)
        axes.set_xlabel("Molares Volumen vₘ [m³/mol]")
        axes.set_ylabel("Molare Helmholtz-Energie fₘ [J/mol]")
        axes.set_title(f"f-v-Isotherme bei T = {tangent.temperature:.2f} K")
        axes.grid(True, alpha=0.25)
        axes.legend(loc="best")
        axes.set_xlim(left=0.0)
        self.figure.tight_layout()
        self.toolbar.update()
        self.canvas.draw_idle()

"""
Datei: fvt_tangent.py
Projekt: ThermoSurface
Kategorie: Thermodynamik / Tangentenkonstruktion

Beschreibung:
Bestimmt die gemeinsame Tangente der f-v-Isotherme aus HEOS-Sättigungsdaten.

Aufgaben:
- Berechnet Sättigungspunkte, Tangentensteigung und Gleichgewichtsprüfung.
- Liefert 2D-Kurvendaten und transformierte 3D-Tangentenpunkte.

Abhängigkeiten:
- Projektmodule (direkt): coolprop_heos_common.py, coolprop_heos_fvt.py,
  diagram_coords.py
- Bibliotheken: CoolProp, numpy

Verwendet von:
- Projektmodule: diagram_viewport.py, fvt_slice_view.py, gui.py
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass

import CoolProp.CoolProp as CP
from CoolProp import AbstractState
import numpy as np

import diagram_coords


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FvtTangent:
    fluid: str
    temperature: float
    p_sat: float
    v_l: float
    v_v: float
    f_l_mass: float
    f_v_mass: float
    f_l_molar: float
    f_v_molar: float
    g_l_mass: float
    g_v_mass: float
    g_l_molar: float
    g_v_molar: float
    mu: float
    mu_heos: float
    line_v: np.ndarray
    line_f_molar: np.ndarray
    tangent_pressure: float
    pressure_relative_error: float
    chemical_potential_delta: float


def tangent_display_points(tangent, fvt_data):
    """Transform molar tangent points using the exact mass-specific f-v-T renderer contract."""
    molar_mass = float(fvt_data["meta"]["molar_mass"])
    if not np.isfinite(molar_mass) or molar_mass <= 0:
        raise ValueError("f-v-T-Daten enthalten keine gueltige molare Masse.")
    native = np.column_stack((
        np.full(len(tangent.line_v), tangent.temperature),
        tangent.line_v,
        tangent.line_f_molar / molar_mass,
    ))
    return diagram_coords.to_xyz_fvt(native, fvt_data["critical_point"])


def tangent_plot_data(tangent):
    """Return linear-axis 2D data and the mathematically extrapolated zero-volume intercept."""
    return {
        "volume": tangent.line_v,
        "helmholtz": tangent.line_f_molar,
        "intercept_volume": 0.0,
        "intercept": tangent.mu,
    }


def homogeneous_fvt_isotherm(tangent, fvt_data, n_points=120):
    """Sample actual HEOS f(v) branches outside the equilibrium two-phase interval."""
    if isinstance(n_points, bool) or int(n_points) != n_points or n_points < 4:
        raise ValueError("n_points muss eine ganze Zahl >= 4 sein.")
    molar_mass = float(fvt_data["meta"]["molar_mass"])
    v_min = float(fvt_data["meta"]["v_min"])
    v_max = float(fvt_data["meta"]["v_max"])
    if not np.isfinite([molar_mass, v_min, v_max]).all() or molar_mass <= 0:
        raise ValueError("f-v-T-Daten enthalten keine gueltige molare Masse oder Volumengrenzen.")
    if not v_min < tangent.v_l < tangent.v_v < v_max:
        raise ValueError("Das f-v-T-Volumenraster schliesst die Sättigungsvolumina nicht ein.")

    from coolprop_heos_common import _new_state
    from coolprop_heos_fvt import _f_at_tv

    state = _new_state(tangent.fluid)
    samples_per_branch = max(2, int(n_points) // 2)
    volumes = (
        np.geomspace(v_min, tangent.v_l * (1.0 - 1e-8), samples_per_branch),
        np.geomspace(tangent.v_v * (1.0 + 1e-8), v_max, samples_per_branch),
    )
    branches = []
    for branch_volumes in volumes:
        energies = []
        valid_volumes = []

        def finish_segment():
            if len(valid_volumes) >= 2:
                branches.append((np.asarray(valid_volumes), np.asarray(energies)))
            valid_volumes.clear()
            energies.clear()

        for volume in branch_volumes:
            try:
                energy = _f_at_tv(state, tangent.temperature, float(volume))[2] * molar_mass
            except ValueError:
                finish_segment()
                continue
            if np.isfinite(energy):
                valid_volumes.append(float(volume))
                energies.append(float(energy))
            else:
                finish_segment()
        finish_segment()
    if not branches:
        raise ValueError("Keine zusammenhängenden homogenen HEOS-Werte verfügbar.")
    return branches


def calculate_fvt_tangent(stoff, T, n_points=100):
    """Calculate saturation endpoints and the physical linear f(v) common tangent."""
    if isinstance(n_points, bool) or int(n_points) != n_points or n_points < 2:
        raise ValueError("n_points muss eine ganze Zahl >= 2 sein.")
    try:
        temperature = float(T)
        state = AbstractState("HEOS", str(stoff))
        critical_temperature = float(state.T_critical())
        minimum_temperature = float(state.Tmin())
    except (ValueError, RuntimeError) as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"HEOS-Stoff oder Temperatur nicht auswertbar: {exc}") from exc

    if not np.isfinite(temperature) or not minimum_temperature < temperature < critical_temperature:
        raise ValueError(f"T={temperature:g} K liegt nicht im unterkritischen Sättigungsbereich "
                         f"({minimum_temperature:g} K < T < {critical_temperature:g} K).")

    phases = []
    try:
        for quality in (0.0, 1.0):
            state.update(CP.QT_INPUTS, quality, temperature)
            phases.append({
                "p": float(state.p()),
                "v": 1.0 / float(state.rhomolar()),
                "f_mass": float(state.helmholtzmass()),
                "g_mass": float(state.gibbsmass()),
                "molar_mass": float(state.molar_mass()),
            })
    except (ValueError, RuntimeError, ZeroDivisionError) as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"HEOS-Sättigungszustand bei T={temperature:g} K fehlgeschlagen: {exc}") from exc

    liquid, vapor = phases
    molar_mass = liquid["molar_mass"]
    values = np.array([liquid["p"], liquid["v"], vapor["v"], liquid["f_mass"], vapor["f_mass"],
                       liquid["g_mass"], vapor["g_mass"], molar_mass], dtype=float)
    if not np.isfinite(values).all() or molar_mass <= 0 or liquid["p"] <= 0 or not liquid["v"] < vapor["v"]:
        raise ValueError("HEOS lieferte unplausible oder nicht endliche Sättigungswerte.")
    if not np.isclose(liquid["p"], vapor["p"], rtol=1e-8, atol=1e-6):
        raise ValueError("HEOS-Flüssigkeits- und Dampfdrücke stimmen nicht überein.")

    f_l = liquid["f_mass"] * molar_mass
    f_v = vapor["f_mass"] * molar_mass
    g_l = liquid["g_mass"] * molar_mass
    g_v = vapor["g_mass"] * molar_mass
    mu = f_l + liquid["p"] * liquid["v"]
    mu_heos = g_l
    potential_tolerance = max(1e-4, abs(mu_heos) * 1e-8, 8.31446261815324 * temperature * 1e-7)
    chemical_potential_delta = max(abs(mu - g_l), abs(mu - g_v), abs(g_l - g_v))
    if chemical_potential_delta > potential_tolerance:
        raise ValueError(f"HEOS-Gleichgewichtspotentiale weichen um {chemical_potential_delta:.6g} J/mol ab "
                         f"(Toleranz {potential_tolerance:.6g} J/mol).")

    tangent_pressure = -(f_v - f_l) / (vapor["v"] - liquid["v"])
    pressure_relative_error = abs(tangent_pressure - liquid["p"]) / liquid["p"]
    if not np.isfinite(pressure_relative_error) or pressure_relative_error > 1e-8:
        raise ValueError(f"Tangentensteigung widerspricht dem HEOS-Sättigungsdruck "
                         f"(relativer Fehler {pressure_relative_error:.6g}).")

    line_v = np.geomspace(liquid["v"], vapor["v"], int(n_points))
    quality = (line_v - liquid["v"]) / (vapor["v"] - liquid["v"])
    return FvtTangent(
        fluid=str(stoff), temperature=temperature, p_sat=liquid["p"], v_l=liquid["v"], v_v=vapor["v"],
        f_l_mass=liquid["f_mass"], f_v_mass=vapor["f_mass"], f_l_molar=f_l, f_v_molar=f_v,
        g_l_mass=liquid["g_mass"], g_v_mass=vapor["g_mass"], g_l_molar=g_l, g_v_molar=g_v,
        mu=mu, mu_heos=mu_heos, line_v=line_v,
        line_f_molar=(1.0 - quality) * f_l + quality * f_v, tangent_pressure=tangent_pressure,
        pressure_relative_error=pressure_relative_error, chemical_potential_delta=chemical_potential_delta)

"""
Datei: geometry_clip.py
Projekt: ThermoSurface
Kategorie: Visualisierung / Geometrie

Beschreibung:
Schneidet vorliegende 3D-Geometrien an einem achsenparallelen Quader.

Aufgaben:
- Clipt Dreiecksflächen und Polylinien und prüft Punktpositionen.
- Kennzeichnet geometrisch interpolierte Schnittpunkte getrennt von Rohdaten.

Abhängigkeiten:
- Projektmodule (direkt): keine
- Bibliotheken: numpy

Verwendet von:
- Projektmodule: diagram_viewport.py
"""

import numpy as np

EPS = 1e-12


def _box_arrays(box):
    box = np.asarray(box, dtype=float)
    if box.shape != (6,) or not np.isfinite(box).all() or np.any(box[0::2] > box[1::2]):
        raise ValueError("Clipping-Box muss (xmin, xmax, ymin, ymax, zmin, zmax) mit min <= max sein.")
    return box[0::2], box[1::2]


def points_in_box(points, box, rtol=1e-9):
    """Boolesche Maske: Punkte innerhalb der Box (mit relativer Toleranz an den Flaechen)."""
    lo, hi = _box_arrays(box)
    pts = np.asarray(points, dtype=float).reshape(-1, 3)
    tol = rtol * np.maximum(hi - lo, 1e-300)
    with np.errstate(invalid="ignore"):
        return np.all((pts >= lo - tol) & (pts <= hi + tol), axis=1)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Dreiecke

def clip_triangles(points, triangles, box, point_data=None):
    """Schneidet Dreiecke an der Box.

    points (n,3), triangles (m,3) Indizes, point_data {name: (n,)-Array} (wird linear interpoliert).
    Rueckgabe dict: points (k,3), triangles (t,3), new (k,) bool (Schnittpunkte), orig_cell (t,) Index des
    Ursprungsdreiecks, point_data {name: (k,)}. Kein Ergebnis -> leere Arrays.
    """
    lo, hi = _box_arrays(box)
    points = np.asarray(points, dtype=float).reshape(-1, 3)
    triangles = np.asarray(triangles, dtype=np.int64).reshape(-1, 3)
    names = list(point_data or {})
    values = {name: np.asarray(point_data[name], dtype=float) for name in names}
    P = points[triangles]                                                  # (m,3,3)
    A = {name: values[name][triangles] for name in names}
    I = triangles.copy()                                                   # Originalindex je Ecke, -1 = neuer Schnittpunkt
    O = np.arange(len(triangles), dtype=np.int64)

    finite = np.isfinite(P).all(axis=(1, 2))
    P, I, O = P[finite], I[finite], O[finite]
    A = {k: v[finite] for k, v in A.items()}

    for axis in range(3):
        for sign, bound in ((1.0, lo[axis]), (-1.0, hi[axis])):
            P, A, I, O = _clip_plane(P, A, I, O, axis, sign, bound, EPS * max(hi[axis] - lo[axis], 1e-300))
            if not len(P):
                break
        if not len(P):
            break

    if len(P):
        edge1, edge2 = P[:, 1] - P[:, 0], P[:, 2] - P[:, 0]
        area2 = np.linalg.norm(np.cross(edge1, edge2), axis=1)
        keep = area2 > 0.0
        P, I, O = P[keep], I[keep], O[keep]
        A = {k: v[keep] for k, v in A.items()}

    if not len(P):
        return {"points": np.empty((0, 3)), "triangles": np.empty((0, 3), dtype=np.int64),
                "new": np.empty(0, dtype=bool), "orig_cell": np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64),
                "point_data": {name: np.empty(0) for name in names}}

    # Unveraenderte Originalknoten behalten ihren Index (schnell); nur die wenigen Schnittpunkte werden
    # (exakt gleiche Koordinaten) untereinander zusammengefuehrt.
    flat_I = I.reshape(-1)
    original = flat_I >= 0
    used = np.unique(flat_I[original])
    remap = np.full(len(points), -1, dtype=np.int64)
    remap[used] = np.arange(len(used))
    new_points = P.reshape(-1, 3)[~original]
    if len(new_points):
        unique_new, first_new, inverse_new = np.unique(new_points, axis=0, return_index=True, return_inverse=True)
        inverse_new = inverse_new.reshape(-1)
    else:
        unique_new, first_new, inverse_new = np.empty((0, 3)), np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64), np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    flat_tri = np.empty(len(flat_I), dtype=np.int64)
    flat_tri[original] = remap[flat_I[original]]
    flat_tri[~original] = len(used) + inverse_new
    out_data = {name: np.concatenate((values[name][used], A[name].reshape(-1)[~original][first_new]))
                for name in names}
    return {"points": np.vstack((points[used], unique_new)), "triangles": flat_tri.reshape(-1, 3),
            "new": np.r_[np.zeros(len(used), bool), np.ones(len(unique_new), bool)], "orig_cell": O,
            "point_data": out_data}


def _clip_plane(P, A, I, O, axis, sign, bound, tol):
    """Behaelt den Halbraum sign*(x[axis]-bound) >= 0; liefert (P, A, I, O) mit neuen Dreiecken."""
    d = sign * (P[:, :, axis] - bound)
    inside = d >= -tol
    count = inside.sum(axis=1)

    keep_all = count == 3
    one = count == 1
    two = count == 2
    out_P, out_I, out_O = [P[keep_all]], [I[keep_all]], [O[keep_all]]
    out_A = {k: [v[keep_all]] for k, v in A.items()}

    def lerp(i_idx, j_idx, rows):
        """Schnittpunkt auf Kante (i drinnen, j draussen) fuer die Dreiecke `rows`."""
        di = d[rows, i_idx]
        dj = d[rows, j_idx]
        t = np.clip(di / (di - dj), 0.0, 1.0)
        pi, pj = P[rows, i_idx], P[rows, j_idx]
        point = pi + t[:, None] * (pj - pi)
        point[:, axis] = bound
        values = {k: v[rows, i_idx] + t * (v[rows, j_idx] - v[rows, i_idx]) for k, v in A.items()}
        return point, values

    if one.any():
        rows = np.flatnonzero(one)
        i = np.argmax(inside[rows], axis=1)                               # einziger innerer Knoten
        j, k = (i + 1) % 3, (i + 2) % 3
        pij, aij = lerp(i, j, rows)
        pik, aik = lerp(i, k, rows)
        none = np.full(len(rows), -1, dtype=np.int64)
        out_P.append(np.stack((P[rows, i], pij, pik), axis=1))
        out_I.append(np.stack((I[rows, i], none, none), axis=1))
        out_O.append(O[rows])
        for name in A:
            out_A[name].append(np.stack((A[name][rows, i], aij[name], aik[name]), axis=1))

    if two.any():
        rows = np.flatnonzero(two)
        k = np.argmax(~inside[rows], axis=1)                              # einziger aeusserer Knoten
        i, j = (k + 1) % 3, (k + 2) % 3
        pjk, ajk = lerp(j, k, rows)
        pki, aki = lerp(i, k, rows)
        none = np.full(len(rows), -1, dtype=np.int64)
        pi_, pj_ = P[rows, i], P[rows, j]
        ii_, ij_ = I[rows, i], I[rows, j]
        out_P.append(np.stack((pi_, pj_, pjk), axis=1))
        out_I.append(np.stack((ii_, ij_, none), axis=1))
        out_P.append(np.stack((pi_, pjk, pki), axis=1))
        out_I.append(np.stack((ii_, none, none), axis=1))
        out_O.extend([O[rows], O[rows]])
        for name in A:
            ai_, aj_ = A[name][rows, i], A[name][rows, j]
            out_A[name].append(np.stack((ai_, aj_, ajk[name]), axis=1))
            out_A[name].append(np.stack((ai_, ajk[name], aki[name]), axis=1))

    return (np.concatenate(out_P), {k: np.concatenate(v) for k, v in out_A.items()},
            np.concatenate(out_I), np.concatenate(out_O))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Kurven

def clip_polylines(points, lines, box):
    """Schneidet Polylinien (Liste von Indexarrays) segmentweise an der Box.

    Rueckgabe dict: points (k,3), lines (Liste von Indexarrays), orig_line (Index der Ursprungslinie je Ergebnislinie).
    Zusammenhaengende, unveraenderte Segmente bleiben eine Polylinie; geschnittene Enden liegen auf der Boxflaeche.
    """
    lo, hi = _box_arrays(box)
    points = np.asarray(points, dtype=float).reshape(-1, 3)
    starts, ends, owner, order = [], [], [], []
    for number, line in enumerate(lines):
        idx = np.asarray(line, dtype=np.int64)
        if len(idx) < 2:
            continue
        starts.append(points[idx[:-1]])
        ends.append(points[idx[1:]])
        owner.append(np.full(len(idx) - 1, number, dtype=np.int64))
        order.append(np.arange(len(idx) - 1, dtype=np.int64))
    empty = {"points": np.empty((0, 3)), "lines": [], "orig_line": np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)}
    if not starts:
        return empty
    a, b = np.concatenate(starts), np.concatenate(ends)
    owner, order = np.concatenate(owner), np.concatenate(order)
    d = b - a
    t0 = np.zeros(len(a))
    t1 = np.ones(len(a))
    alive = np.isfinite(a).all(axis=1) & np.isfinite(b).all(axis=1)
    tol = EPS * np.maximum(hi - lo, 1e-300)
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        for axis in range(3):
            for p, q in ((-d[:, axis], a[:, axis] - lo[axis]), (d[:, axis], hi[axis] - a[:, axis])):
                parallel = p == 0.0
                alive &= ~(parallel & (q < -tol[axis]))
                r = np.where(parallel, 0.0, q / np.where(parallel, 1.0, p))
                entering = (p < 0) & ~parallel
                leaving = (p > 0) & ~parallel
                t0 = np.where(entering, np.maximum(t0, r), t0)
                t1 = np.where(leaving, np.minimum(t1, r), t1)
    alive &= (t1 - t0) > EPS
    kept = np.flatnonzero(alive)
    if not len(kept):
        return empty

    pa = a[kept] + t0[kept, None] * d[kept]
    pb = a[kept] + t1[kept, None] * d[kept]
    clipped_a = t0[kept] > 0.0
    clipped_b = t1[kept] < 1.0
    pa[clipped_a] = np.clip(pa[clipped_a], lo, hi)
    pb[clipped_b] = np.clip(pb[clipped_b], lo, hi)
    owner_k, order_k = owner[kept], order[kept]
    joined = np.zeros(len(kept), dtype=bool)
    joined[1:] = ((owner_k[1:] == owner_k[:-1]) & (order_k[1:] == order_k[:-1] + 1)
                  & ~clipped_b[:-1] & ~clipped_a[1:])

    out_points, out_lines, out_owner = [], [], []
    current = None
    for n in range(len(kept)):
        if not joined[n]:
            if current is not None:
                out_lines.append(np.asarray(current, dtype=np.int64))
            current = [len(out_points)]
            out_points.append(pa[n])
            out_owner.append(owner_k[n])
        out_points.append(pb[n])
        current.append(len(out_points) - 1)
    out_lines.append(np.asarray(current, dtype=np.int64))
    return {"points": np.asarray(out_points), "lines": out_lines, "orig_line": np.asarray(out_owner, dtype=np.int64)}

class ThermoSurfaceWindow(QMainWindow):

    def __init__(self, start_stoff, surface_cfg, raster, visu_raster, berechnen, grid_generator, debug_mode=False,
                 isoline_defaults=None):
        super().__init__()

        self.base_raster = deepcopy(raster)
        self.base_visu_raster = deepcopy(visu_raster)

        # TS-VP03/04: Isolinien-Vorgaben und Ansichtsbereiche je Diagrammtyp (ueberleben das Neuerzeugen von Viewports).
        self._isoline_defaults = deepcopy(isoline_defaults) if isoline_defaults else {}
        self._isoline_prefs = {}
        self._isoline_target_id = None
        self._view_prefs = {}
        self._view_edit = {}

        # Fehlende Zusatzfelder der alten config.yaml ergänzen (Fallback: linear).
        for groups in (self.base_raster, self.base_visu_raster):
            for name, cfg in groups.items():

                if name in ("isotherm_grid", "isotherm_visu", "isochore_grid", "isochore_visu"):
                    cfg.setdefault("v_spacing", "linear")
                    cfg.setdefault("v_points", 1000)

        # Druckgrenzen der p-v-T-Zweiphasenfläche (Pa); alte config.yaml ohne Grenzen: kein Beschnitt.
        self.base_visu_raster.setdefault("two_phase_visu", {}).setdefault("p_start_twophase", 1.0e3)
        self.base_visu_raster["two_phase_visu"].setdefault("p_stop_twophase", 1.0e10)

        self.confirmed_stoff = start_stoff
        self.auto_defaults = None
        self.grid_generator = grid_generator
        self.berechnen = berechnen
        self.pvt_grid_stoff = start_stoff

        self.debug_mode = debug_mode
        self._requested_ids = []

        # Berechnungsdaten bleiben ueber Moduswechsel hinweg im Cache (keine Kameradaten im Schluessel).
        self.cache = DiagramResultCache(max_bytes=CACHE_MAX_BYTES, warn_bytes=CACHE_WARN_BYTES)
        self.manager = CalculationManager(berechnen, self.cache, self)
        self.manager.progress.connect(self._update_progress)
        self.manager.finished.connect(self._batch_finished)

        # Layer-Vorgaben je Diagrammtyp; sie ueberleben das Neuerzeugen eines Viewports.
        self._layer_prefs = {}
        self._grid_prefs = {}
        self.mode = MODE_SINGLE

        # Zustandsvergleich (Implementierung B): inaktiv = keine Zustandsauswertung, kein Plattenzugriff.
        try:
            import CoolProp
            coolprop_version = CoolProp.__version__
        except Exception:
            coolprop_version = "n/a"
        self.state_store = StateStore(StateStoreConfig(**STATE_CACHE), coolprop_version=coolprop_version)
        self.resolver = StateResolver(self.state_store)
        self.selection = SelectionManager()
        self.selection.subscribe(self._on_selection_event)
        self.comparison_view = None
        self._projection_enabled = False
        self._projection_target_id = "pvt"
        self._projection_pending = False
        self._fvt_tangent_temperatures = {}
        self._fvt_tangent_result = None
        self._fvt_slice_dock = None
        self._fvt_slice_view = None
        self._fvt_isotherm_sampler = None
        self._tangent_timer = QTimer(self)
        self._tangent_timer.setSingleShot(True)
        self._tangent_timer.setInterval(130)
        self._tangent_timer.timeout.connect(self._update_fvt_tangent)

        self.setWindowTitle("ThermoSurface – 3D-Zustandsdiagramme")

        # 1. Hauptfenster aufteilen
        splitter = QSplitter(Qt.Orientation.Horizontal)

        self.controls = QWidget()
        self.viewer_container = QWidget()

        splitter.addWidget(self.controls)
        splitter.addWidget(self.viewer_container)

        # Seitenleiste soll bedienbar bleiben, der 3D-Viewport flexibel wachsen.
        self.controls.setMinimumWidth(360)
        splitter.setChildrenCollapsible(False)
        splitter.setStretchFactor(0, 0)
        splitter.setStretchFactor(1, 1)
        splitter.setSizes([480, 920])

        self.setCentralWidget(splitter)

        # 2. Layouts erstellen
        self.controls_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.controls)
        self.controls_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.controls_layout.setSpacing(0)
        self.viewer_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.viewer_container)

        # 3. Workspace (Viewports) vor den Steuerungselementen anlegen
        self.setup_viewer()

        # 4. Steuerungselemente erstellen
        self.setup_controls(start_stoff, surface_cfg)

        # 5. Fenstergröße festlegen
        self.resize(1400, 850)


    def setup_controls(self, start_stoff, surface_cfg):
        scroll = QScrollArea()
        scroll.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAsNeeded)
        scroll.setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarPolicy.ScrollBarAsNeeded)
        content = QWidget()
        panel = QVBoxLayout(content)
        panel.setContentsMargins(8, 8, 8, 8)
        panel.setSpacing(6)
        scroll.setWidget(content)
        self.controls_layout.addWidget(scroll)

        model_group = QGroupBox("Modellkonfiguration")
        model_form = QFormLayout(model_group)
        model_form.setRowWrapPolicy(QFormLayout.RowWrapPolicy.WrapLongRows)
        model_form.setFieldGrowthPolicy(QFormLayout.FieldGrowthPolicy.AllNonFixedFieldsGrow)
        model_form.setContentsMargins(8, 7, 8, 7)
        model_form.setHorizontalSpacing(6)
        model_form.setVerticalSpacing(4)
        panel.addWidget(model_group)

        self.stoff_combo = QComboBox()
        self.stoff_combo.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)

        for title, code in (
            ("Wasser", "Water"), 
            ("CO₂", "CO2"), 
            ("Methan", "Methane"),
            ("n-Propane", "n-Propane"), 
            ("Argon", "Argon"),
            ("Stickstoff", "Nitrogen"), 
            ("Ammoniak", "Ammonia"),
            ("Ethanol", "Ethanol"), 
            ("R134a - Kältemittel", "R134a"),
            ("n-Hexane", "n-Hexane")):

            self.stoff_combo.addItem(title, code)

        index = self.stoff_combo.findData(start_stoff)

        if index < 0:
            self.stoff_combo.addItem(start_stoff, start_stoff)
            index = self.stoff_combo.count() - 1

        self.stoff_combo.setCurrentIndex(index)

        # Darstellung: Einzel (Standard, bisherige Bedienung) oder Mehrfach (Checkboxen).
        self.mode_combo = QComboBox()
        self.mode_combo.addItem("Einzel", MODE_SINGLE)
        self.mode_combo.addItem("Mehrfach", MODE_MULTI)
        model_form.addRow("Darstellung:", self.mode_combo)

        model_form.addRow("Stoff (Auswahl):", self.stoff_combo)
        self.confirm_button = QPushButton("Stoff festlegen")
        self.reset_button = QPushButton("Automatische Werte zurücksetzen")
        model_form.addRow(self.confirm_button)
        model_form.addRow(self.reset_button)

        self.diagram_combo = QComboBox()
        for spec in DIAGRAMS:
            self.diagram_combo.addItem(spec.label, spec.id)
        model_form.addRow("Diagrammtyp:", self.diagram_combo)

        # Mehrfachauswahl: eine Checkbox je registriertem Diagramm (mindestens eines erforderlich).
        self.diagram_checks_widget = QWidget()
        checks_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.diagram_checks_widget)
        checks_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        checks_layout.setSpacing(2)
        self.diagram_checks = {}
        for spec in DIAGRAMS:
            box = QCheckBox(spec.label)
            box.setChecked(spec.id == "pvt")
            box.toggled.connect(self._on_selection_changed)
            self.diagram_checks[spec.id] = box
            checks_layout.addWidget(box)
        model_form.addRow("Diagramme:", self.diagram_checks_widget)
        self._model_form = model_form
        model_form.setRowVisible(self.diagram_checks_widget, False)
        self.mode_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_mode_changed)

        self.critical_info = QLabel("Startwerte aus config.yaml; noch nicht automatisch angepasst")
        self.critical_info.setWordWrap(True)
        model_form.addRow("Stoffdaten:", self.critical_info)
        self.confirm_button.clicked.connect(self.on_confirm_stoff)
        self.reset_button.clicked.connect(self.on_reset_auto)

        # Nur Parameter anzeigen, die die aktuell gewählte Darstellung steuern.
        titles = {
            "isotherm_visu":    "Darstellung: Isothermen",
            "isochore_visu":   "Darstellung: Isochoren",
            "isobare_visu":    "Darstellung: Isobaren",
            "binodale_visu":    "Darstellung: Binodale und Zweiphasenfläche",
            "spinodale_visu":   "Darstellung: Spinodale",
            "two_phase_visu":   "Darstellung: Zweiphasenauflösung",}

        self.pvt_parameter_groups = []
        self.grid_fields = {"grid_points_creation": {}, "visu_points_creation": {}}
        surface_parameters = self.base_raster["isotherm_grid"]
        surface_group = QGroupBox("Berechnung: Zustandsfläche")
        surface_form = QFormLayout(surface_group)
        surface_form.setRowWrapPolicy(QFormLayout.RowWrapPolicy.WrapAllRows)
        surface_form.setFieldGrowthPolicy(QFormLayout.FieldGrowthPolicy.AllNonFixedFieldsGrow)
        surface_form.setContentsMargins(8, 7, 8, 7)
        surface_form.setHorizontalSpacing(4)
        surface_form.setVerticalSpacing(3)
        panel.addWidget(surface_group)
        self.pvt_parameter_groups.append(surface_group)
        surface_fields = {}
        for key in ("T_start_isotherm", "T_stop_isotherm", "T_step_isotherm"):
            control = self._make_grid_field(key, surface_parameters[key])
            control.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
            label = QLabel(self._grid_label(key))
            label.setWordWrap(True)
            surface_form.addRow(label, control)
            surface_fields[key] = control
        self.grid_fields["grid_points_creation"]["isotherm_grid"] = surface_fields

        section = "visu_points_creation"
        for group_name, parameters in self.base_visu_raster.items():
            full_title = titles.get(group_name, group_name)
            display_title = ("Darstellung: Binodale / Zweiphasenfläche"
                             if group_name == "binodale_visu" else full_title)
            widget_group = QGroupBox(display_title)
            form = QFormLayout(widget_group)
            form.setRowWrapPolicy(QFormLayout.RowWrapPolicy.WrapAllRows)
            form.setFieldGrowthPolicy(QFormLayout.FieldGrowthPolicy.AllNonFixedFieldsGrow)
            form.setContentsMargins(8, 7, 8, 7)
            form.setHorizontalSpacing(4)
            form.setVerticalSpacing(3)
            panel.addWidget(widget_group)
            self.pvt_parameter_groups.append(widget_group)

            fields = {}
            display_parameters = dict(parameters)
            if group_name == "isotherm_visu":
                start = float(parameters["T_start_isotherm"])
                stop = float(parameters["T_stop_isotherm"])
                step = float(parameters["T_step_isotherm"])
                display_parameters.pop("T_step_isotherm")
                display_parameters["n_isotherms"] = max(
                    2, int(np.ceil((stop - start) / step)))

            for key, value in display_parameters.items():
                control = self._make_grid_field(key, value)
                control.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Policy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Policy.Fixed)
                label = QLabel(self._grid_label(key))
                label.setWordWrap(True)
                form.addRow(label, control)
                fields[key] = control

            if group_name == "isotherm_visu" and "n_isotherms" in fields:
                # TS-VP03: Die Isothermenwerte folgen der geometrischen Progression (Gruppe "Isolinien"); die Anzahl
                # ergibt sich aus Start, Stop und Faktor. Das Feld bleibt nur fuer die Rasterkompatibilitaet bestehen.
                fields["n_isotherms"].setEnabled(False)
                fields["n_isotherms"].setToolTip("Ersetzt durch die Isolinien-Progression (Start, Stop, Faktor).")

            self.grid_fields[section][group_name] = fields

        layer_group = QGroupBox("Darstellungsebenen")
        layer_layout = QVBoxLayout(layer_group)
        layer_layout.setContentsMargins(8, 7, 8, 7)
        layer_layout.setSpacing(3)
        self.layer_checks = {}

        for key, label in LAYER_LABELS.items():
            checkbox = QCheckBox(label)
            self.layer_checks[key] = checkbox
            layer_layout.addWidget(checkbox)
            checkbox.toggled.connect(lambda checked, k=key: self._on_layer_toggled(k, checked))

        self.export_button = QPushButton("Homogene Flächen exportieren (CSV)")
        self.export_button.setToolTip(
            "Exportiert die vorhandenen homogenen Flächen; die source-Spalte kennzeichnet die Datenherkunft.")
        self.export_button.setEnabled(False)
        layer_layout.addWidget(self.export_button)
        self.export_button.clicked.connect(self.on_export_homogeneous)

        # Checkbox für das Oberflächengitter (gilt für das aktive Diagramm)
        self.grid_checkbox = QCheckBox("Oberflächengitter anzeigen")
        self.grid_checkbox.setChecked(True)

        layer_layout.addWidget(self.grid_checkbox)

        # Signal mit Funktion verbinden
        self.grid_checkbox.toggled.connect(self.on_surface_grid_toggled)

        # Berechnungssteuerung als eigene Gruppe; derselbe Button/Callback.
        calculate_group = QGroupBox("Berechnung starten")
        calculate_layout = QVBoxLayout(calculate_group)
        calculate_layout.setContentsMargins(8, 7, 8, 7)
        self.calculate_button = QPushButton("Berechnung starten")
        calculate_layout.addWidget(self.calculate_button)

        # Sichtbarer Status und prozentualer Fortschritt unter dem Button
        self.progress_bar = QProgressBar()
        self.progress_bar.setRange(0, 100)
        self.progress_bar.setValue(0)
        self.progress_bar.setFormat("%p %")
        self.progress_bar.setTextVisible(True)
        calculate_layout.addWidget(self.progress_bar)

        self.progress_label = QLabel("Bereit")
        self.progress_label.setWordWrap(True)
        calculate_layout.addWidget(self.progress_label)

        # Wichtig: Parametergruppen existieren bereits. Nur die zwei
        # priorisierten Gruppen werden im Layout VOR ihnen eingefügt.
        panel.insertWidget(1, calculate_group)
        panel.insertWidget(2, layer_group)
        panel.insertWidget(3, self._build_view_group())
        panel.insertWidget(4, self._build_isoline_group())
        panel.insertWidget(5, self._build_compare_group())
        panel.insertWidget(6, self._build_fvt_tangent_group())
        panel.addStretch()
        self.calculate_button.clicked.connect(self.on_calculate_clicked)

        self.diagram_combo.currentIndexChanged.connect(self.on_diagram_changed)
        self.on_diagram_changed()
        self._refresh_fvt_tangent_panel()
        self.controls.setMinimumWidth(max(
            self.controls.minimumWidth(),
            content.minimumSizeHint().width() + scroll.verticalScrollBar().sizeHint().width() + 2 * scroll.frameWidth()))

    @staticmethod
    def _grid_label(key):
        labels = {
            "T_start_isotherm": "Temperatur von [K]",
            "T_stop_isotherm": "Temperatur bis [K]",
            "T_step_isotherm": "Temperaturabstand [K]",
            "n_isotherms": "Anzahl Isothermen",
            "T_start_isochore": "Temperatur von [K]",
            "T_stop_isochore": "Temperatur bis [K]",
            "T_start_isobare": "Temperatur von [K]",
            "T_stop_isobare": "Temperatur bis [K]",
            "T_start_sat": "Temperatur von [K]",
            "T_stop_sat": "Temperatur bis [K]",
            "T_start_spinodal": "Temperatur von [K]",
            "T_stop_spinodal": "Temperatur bis [K]",
            "v_start_isochore": "Volumen von [m³/mol]",
            "v_stop_isochore": "Volumen bis [m³/mol]",
            "v_start_isotherm": "Volumen von [m³/mol]",
            "v_stop_isotherm": "Volumen bis [m³/mol]",
            "T_step_isochore": "Temperaturabstand [K]",
            "T_step_isobare": "Temperaturabstand [K]",
            "T_step_sat": "Temperaturabstand [K]",
            "v_step_isochore": "Volumenabstand [m³/mol]",
            "v_step_isotherm": "Volumenabstand [m³/mol]",
            "p_step_isobare": "Druckabstand [Pa]",
        }
        if key in labels:
            return labels[key]
        if key == "v_spacing":
            return "Volumenraster (linear/log)"
        if key == "v_points":
            return "v-Punkte (nur log)"
        if key == "Q_points":
            return "Dampfgehalt Q: Punkte"
        if key == "p_start_twophase":
            return "Zweiphasenfläche (p-v-T): untere Druckgrenze [Pa]"
        if key == "p_stop_twophase":
            return "Zweiphasenfläche (p-v-T): obere Druckgrenze [Pa]"
        if key == "n_T":
            return "Spinodale: Temperaturpunkte"
        if key == "n_scan":
            return "Spinodale: Dichtepunkte (adaptive Suche)"
        if key == "residual_tolerance":
            return "Spinodale: interne Residualtoleranz"
        if key.startswith("v_"):
            return key + " [m³/mol]" + (" (nur linear)" if "_step_" in key else "")
        if key.startswith("T_"):
            return key + " [K]"
        if key.startswith("p_"):
            return key + " [Pa]"
        return key

    @staticmethod
    def _make_grid_field(key, value):
        if key == "v_spacing":
            field = QComboBox()
            field.addItem("Linear", "linear")
            field.addItem("Logarithmisch", "log")
            field.setCurrentIndex(field.findData(value))
            return field

        if key in ("v_points", "Q_points", "n_T", "n_scan", "n_isotherms"):
            field = QSpinBox()
            if key in ("n_T", "n_isotherms"):
                field.setRange(2, 10000)
            elif key == "n_scan":
                field.setRange(16, 10000)
            else:
                field.setRange(2, MAX_POINTS)

            field.setValue(int(value))

            return field

        field = QDoubleSpinBox()
        if key == "residual_tolerance":
            field.setDecimals(12)
            field.setRange(1e-12, 1e-2)
            field.setSingleStep(1e-8)
        elif key.startswith("v_"):
            field.setDecimals(12)
            field.setRange(1e-12, 100000.0)
            field.setSingleStep(1e-6)
        elif key.startswith("p_"):
            field.setDecimals(3)
            field.setRange(1e-3, 1e11)
            field.setSingleStep(1e5)
        else:
            field.setDecimals(6)
            field.setRange(1e-6, 1e6)
            field.setSingleStep(0.1)
        field.setValue(float(value))

        return field

    def _read_grids(self):
        raster = deepcopy(self.base_raster)
        visu_raster = deepcopy(self.base_visu_raster)
        for name, controls in self.grid_fields["grid_points_creation"].items():
            params = raster[name]
            for key, field in controls.items():
                params[key] = field.value()

        for name, controls in self.grid_fields["visu_points_creation"].items():
            params = visu_raster[name]
            for key, field in controls.items():
                params[key] = field.currentData() if key == "v_spacing" else field.value()
            if name == "isotherm_visu":
                count = int(params.pop("n_isotherms"))
                params["T_step_isotherm"] = (
                    params["T_stop_isotherm"] - params["T_start_isotherm"]
                ) / (count - 1)
                params["n_isotherms"] = count

        return raster, visu_raster

    def _write_grids(self, raster, visu_raster):
        for section, groups, source in (
                ("grid_points_creation", self.grid_fields["grid_points_creation"], raster),
                ("visu_points_creation", self.grid_fields["visu_points_creation"], visu_raster)):
            for name, controls in groups.items():
                params = source[name]
                for key, field in controls.items():
                    if key == "n_isotherms":
                        start = float(params["T_start_isotherm"])
                        stop = float(params["T_stop_isotherm"])
                        step = float(params["T_step_isotherm"])
                        value = max(2, int(np.ceil((stop - start) / step)))
                    else:
                        value = params[key]
                    if key == "v_spacing":
                        field.setCurrentIndex(field.findData(value))
                    else:
                        field.setValue(value)

    
    def selected_diagram_ids(self):
        """Ausgewaehlte Diagramme in Registry-Reihenfolge (Einzel: genau eines, Mehrfach: Checkboxen)."""
        if self.mode == MODE_SINGLE:
            return [self.diagram_combo.currentData()]
        return [spec.id for spec in DIAGRAMS if self.diagram_checks[spec.id].isChecked()]

    def on_confirm_stoff(self):

        stoff = self.stoff_combo.currentData()
        ids = self.selected_diagram_ids()

        # Nur p-v-T benoetigt die automatische Rasteranpassung; alle anderen Diagramme
        # erstellen ihr eigenes Raster bei Berechnungsstart.
        if "pvt" not in ids:
            self.confirmed_stoff = stoff
            self.selection.clear_for_fluid_change(stoff)
            label = DIAGRAMS.get(ids[0]).confirm_label if len(ids) == 1 else "Mehrfachansicht"
            self.critical_info.setText(f"{stoff}: Stoff übernommen. " f"{label}-Raster wird beim Berechnungsstart erzeugt.")
            self.statusBar().showMessage(f"{stoff} übernommen; " f"{label}-Berechnung noch nicht gestartet.")

            return

        # p-v-T:
        # Bisherige automatische Rasteranpassung beibehalten.
        try:
            raster, visu, meta = self.grid_generator(stoff, self.base_raster, self.base_visu_raster)

        except Exception as exc:
            if self.debug_mode:
                LOGGER.exception("Automatische Stoffanpassung fehlgeschlagen")
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Stoff konnte nicht übernommen werden", str(exc))

            return

        self.auto_defaults = deepcopy((raster, visu, meta))
        self.base_raster = deepcopy(raster)
        self.base_visu_raster = deepcopy(visu)
        self._write_grids(raster, visu)

        self.confirmed_stoff = stoff
        self.pvt_grid_stoff = stoff
        self.selection.clear_for_fluid_change(stoff)
        self.critical_info.setText(f"{stoff}: Tc={meta['Tc']:.3f} K, " f"pc={meta['pc']/1e6:.3f} MPa, " f"vc={meta['vc']:.8g} m³/mol, " f"Ttr={meta['Ttr']:.3f} K")

        self.statusBar().showMessage(f"Raster für {stoff} angepasst; ""Berechnung noch nicht gestartet.")

    def on_reset_auto(self):
        if "pvt" not in self.selected_diagram_ids():
            return
        
        if self.auto_defaults is None:
            # Beim ersten Zurücksetzen die aktuell bestätigte Stoffauswahl verwenden.
            current_index = self.stoff_combo.currentIndex()
            confirmed_index = self.stoff_combo.findData(self.confirmed_stoff)
            self.stoff_combo.setCurrentIndex(confirmed_index)
            self.on_confirm_stoff()
            self.stoff_combo.setCurrentIndex(current_index)
            return
        
        raster, visu, _meta = self.auto_defaults
        self.base_raster = deepcopy(raster)
        self.base_visu_raster = deepcopy(visu)
        self._write_grids(raster, visu)
        self.statusBar().showMessage("Automatische Standardwerte wiederhergestellt.")

    def get_settings(self):
        raster, visu_raster = self._read_grids()
        ids = self.selected_diagram_ids()
        viewport = self.workspace.active
        layers = viewport.effective_layers() if viewport is not None else {}
        return {
            "stoff": self.confirmed_stoff,
            "diagram_type": ids[0] if ids else None,
            "diagram_types": ids,
            "raster": raster,
            "visu_raster": visu_raster,
            "layers": layers,}

    def on_calculate_clicked(self):

        # Keine parallelen Berechnungen
        if self.manager.is_running:
            return

        # Mehrfachansicht braucht mindestens ein angekreuztes Diagramm
        if not self.selected_diagram_ids():
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Kein Diagramm ausgewählt", "Bitte mindestens ein Diagramm ankreuzen.")
            self.statusBar().showMessage("Berechnung abgewiesen: kein Diagramm ausgewählt.")
            return

        # Stoff muss zuerst bestätigt werden
        if self.stoff_combo.currentData() != self.confirmed_stoff:

            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Stoffauswahl nicht bestätigt", "Bitte zuerst 'Stoff festlegen' wählen.")
            return

        settings = self.get_settings()
        ids = settings["diagram_types"]

        # Verhindert die Verwendung eines p-v-T-Rasters,
        # das noch zu einem zuvor bestätigten Stoff gehört.
        if ("pvt" in ids and self.pvt_grid_stoff != self.confirmed_stoff):
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "p-v-T-Raster aktualisieren","Bitte zuerst 'Stoff festlegen' wählen, ""damit die p-v-T-Raster angepasst werden.")
            return

        stoff, raster, visu_raster = self.prepare_calculation(settings)

        isolines, isoline_error = self._validate_isolines(ids)
        if isoline_error:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Ungültige Isolinienvorgabe", isoline_error)
            self.statusBar().showMessage(f"Berechnung abgewiesen: {isoline_error}")
            return

        # Auswahl der gestarteten Berechnung festhalten; sie darf waehrend des QThread-Laufs nicht wechseln.
        self._requested_ids = list(ids)
        tasks = [DiagramTask(diagram_id, stoff, deepcopy(raster), deepcopy(visu_raster),
                             isolines=deepcopy(isolines[diagram_id]) if diagram_id in self._isoline_prefs else None)
                 for diagram_id in ids]
        self._set_busy(True)
        self.progress_bar.setValue(0)
        self.progress_label.setText("Berechnung wird vorbereitet ...")
        self.statusBar().showMessage(f"Berechne {', '.join(ids)} für {stoff} ...")
        if not self.manager.start(tasks):
            self._set_busy(False)

    def _set_busy(self, busy):
        for widget in (self.calculate_button, self.confirm_button, self.stoff_combo,
                       self.diagram_combo, self.mode_combo, self.diagram_checks_widget):
            widget.setEnabled(not busy)
        self.reset_button.setEnabled(not busy and "pvt" in self.selected_diagram_ids())

    @Slot(int, str)
    def _update_progress(self, percent, phase):
        self.progress_bar.setValue(int(percent))
        self.progress_label.setText(phase)
        self.statusBar().showMessage(phase)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ Ergebnisverarbeitung
    @Slot(object)
    def _batch_finished(self, batch):
        """Alle Diagramme der Warteschlange sind fertig: Ansicht aufbauen, Fehler je Diagramm melden."""
        self._set_busy(False)
        self.progress_bar.setValue(96)
        self.progress_label.setText("Erstelle 3D-Visualisierung ...")
        self.statusBar().showMessage("Erstelle 3D-Visualisierung ...")

        errors = [(o.task.label, o.error) for o in batch.failed]
        viewports = self._display_outcomes(batch.succeeded, errors)

        status_parts = []
        info_failed = {label for label, _ in errors}
        for outcome in batch.succeeded:
            if outcome.task.label in info_failed or outcome.task.diagram_id not in viewports:
                continue
            status = f"{outcome.task.diagram_id} abgeschlossen: {self.confirmed_stoff}"
            if outcome.from_cache:
                status += " (aus Cache)"
            report = outcome.daten.get("isoline_report") if isinstance(outcome.daten, dict) else None
            if report:
                status += " | Isolinien T/v/p: " + "/".join(str(report[f]["n_levels"]) for f in ISOLINE_FAMILIES)
            if outcome.task.diagram_id == "pvt":
                status, tooltips, progress_text = self._pvt_report(outcome.daten, status)
                viewports["pvt"].tooltips = tooltips
                if progress_text:
                    self.progress_label.setText(progress_text)
            status_parts.append(status)

        if self.cache.warnings:
            status_parts.append(self.cache.warnings[-1])
            self.cache.warnings.clear()
        if self.cache.evictions:
            status_parts.append(f"{len(self.cache.evictions)} ältere Ergebnis(se) aus dem Cache verdrängt (werden bei Bedarf neu berechnet)")
            self.cache.evictions.clear()

        if errors:
            self.progress_label.setText("Teilweise fehlgeschlagen: " + ", ".join(label for label, _ in errors))
            self._report_errors(errors, shown=len(viewports))
        elif viewports:
            self.progress_bar.setValue(100)
            if not self.progress_label.text().startswith("Druckachse"):
                self.progress_label.setText("Berechnung und Darstellung abgeschlossen")
        if self._projection_enabled:
            target_viewport = self.workspace.viewports.get(self._projection_target_id)
            if target_viewport is not None and target_viewport.has_result:
                self.workspace.set_active(target_viewport)
        self._refresh_layer_panel()
        self._refresh_view_panel()
        self._refresh_isoline_panel()
        self._refresh_fvt_tangent_panel()
        self._sync_markers()
        self.on_diagram_changed(preserve_status=True)
        if status_parts:
            self.statusBar().showMessage(" | ".join(status_parts))
        elif errors:
            self.statusBar().showMessage("Fehler")
        if self._projection_enabled and self._projection_pending:
            self._projection_pending = False
            QTimer.singleShot(0, self._ensure_projection_target)

    def _report_errors(self, errors, shown):
        lines = "\n".join(f"• {label}: {message}" for label, message in errors)
        if self.mode == MODE_SINGLE or shown == 0:
            QMessageBox.critical(self, "Berechnung fehlgeschlagen", lines)
        else:
            QMessageBox.warning(self, "Diagramme übersprungen",
                                "Folgende Diagramme konnten nicht erstellt werden und wurden übersprungen; "
                                "alle anderen sind verfügbar:\n" + lines)

    def _display_outcomes(self, outcomes, errors):
        """Zeigt erfolgreiche Ergebnisse im Workspace; Renderfehler werden wie Rechenfehler isoliert."""
        if not outcomes:
            return {}

        # Layer-/Gittereinstellungen aller bestehenden Viewports sichern (je Diagrammtyp).
        self._remember_viewport_state()
        viewports, created = self.workspace.ensure([o.task.diagram_id for o in outcomes])
        for diagram_id in created:
            viewport = viewports[diagram_id]
            viewport.set_layer_state(self._layer_prefs.get(diagram_id, {}))
            viewport.grid_on = self._grid_prefs.get(diagram_id, True)
            viewport.tooltips = {}
            # Neu gebildete Viewports desselben Typs uebernehmen den gespeicherten Ansichtsbereich.
            if diagram_id in self._view_prefs:
                viewport.view_state = self._view_prefs[diagram_id].copy()

        for outcome in outcomes:
            diagram_id = outcome.task.diagram_id
            viewport = viewports[diagram_id]
            if can_skip_render(viewport.has_result, viewport.result_key, outcome.key, viewport.render_state):
                LOGGER.debug("event=render_cache_skip diagram=%s viewport=%x key=%s",
                             diagram_id, id(viewport), outcome.key)
                continue
            if viewport.result_key == outcome.key and viewport.render_state == FAILED:
                viewport = self.workspace.replace_viewport(diagram_id)
                viewports[diagram_id] = viewport
            try:
                viewport.show_result(outcome.daten, outcome.key)
                self._after_render(viewport, outcome)
            except Exception as exc:
                LOGGER.exception("Visualisierung %s fehlgeschlagen", diagram_id)
                errors.append((outcome.task.label, f"Darstellung fehlgeschlagen: {exc}"))
                self.workspace.ensure([i for i in viewports if i != diagram_id])
                viewports = dict(self.workspace.viewports)
        return viewports

    def _after_render(self, viewport, outcome):
        viewport.info_text = viewport.spec.info_text(outcome.daten, self.confirmed_stoff)
        if self.debug_mode and viewport.diagram_id == "pvt":
            from Debug import diagnose_visualisierung
            from visualization import to_xyz

            daten = outcome.daten
            diagnose_visualisierung(
                daten, viewport.actors,
                lambda points, critical: to_xyz(points, critical, daten.get("pressure_axis", "log")))

    def _remember_viewport_state(self):
        for viewport in self.workspace.ordered():
            self._layer_prefs[viewport.diagram_id] = dict(viewport.layer_state)
            self._grid_prefs[viewport.diagram_id] = viewport.grid_on
            self._view_prefs[viewport.diagram_id] = viewport.view_state.copy()

    def _pvt_report(self, daten, status):
        """AP3-Statusmeldungen und Tooltips fuer p-v-T; Rueckgabe (status, tooltips, progress_text)."""
        tooltips = {}
        progress_text = None
        if isinstance(daten, dict):
            # AP3: Unvollstaendige oder nicht gepruefte Spinodalen nicht als
            # uneingeschraenkt validiert in der GUI praesentieren.
